### [1] [Sensitivity of the SHiP experiment at CERN to sterile-neutrino-induced oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18824)
*SHiP Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SHiP实验利用双基线探测器配置，通过近远端探测器测量中微子振荡，以探测轻质量（eV量级）的第四代（类惰性）中微子；基于Geant4/GENIE模拟与统计分析，给出了在6×10²⁰ POT下对|U_μ4|²和|U_e4|²的95% CL灵敏度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测轻质量（~eV）的惰性中微子，解释LSND、MiniBooNE等实验中的异常信号，并检验3+1振荡模型；SHiP高统计量、双基线几何构型为其提供独特灵敏度。

Method: 采用Geant4模拟SHiP探测器响应，输入GENIE生成的νₑ、ν_μ带电流失散事例及π⁰中性流背景；构建近/远端事例率比值分析框架，计入20%通量形状不确定性和1%远/近归一化不确定性，执行ν_μ与νₑ消失振荡拟合。

Result: 在6×10²⁰ POT下，95% CL中位灵敏度达：|U_μ4|² ≈ 1.2×10⁻⁴（对应m₄ ≈ 0.4 eV），|U_e4|² ≈ 2.5×10⁻³（对应m₄ ≈ 0.15 eV）；其中μ子通道在0.2–1.0 eV质量区间优于现有ν_μ消失实验限制。

Conclusion: SHiP作为高强束流下的双基线中微子实验，具备世界领先的eV尺度惰性中微子探测能力，尤其在ν_μ消失道可显著拓展参数空间，为解决短基线中微子异常提供关键检验。

Abstract: The SHiP experiment, approved for the high-intensity beam-dump facility in the ECN3 hall at the CERN SPS in the North Area, will record neutrino interactions both in its scattering and neutrino detector (SND) and, about \SI{70}{m} further downstream, in its calorimeter system. We show that the baseline SHiP detector configuration therefore forms a two-baseline experiment for oscillations induced by a sterile neutrino. From a \textsc{Geant4} simulation of \textsc{Genie}-generated interactions we derive the energy response of both stations to $\nue$ and $\numu$ charged-current (CC) interactions and the separation of $\nue$~CC events from the neutral-current $\pizero$ background, and fold them into a near/far disappearance analysis with a $20\%$ flux-shape uncertainty and a $1\%$ far/near normalisation uncertainty. For $6\times10^{20}$ protons on target, the projected $95\%$~CL median sensitivities are $\Umufour\simeq\UmuLimit$ at $\mfour\simeq\UmuLimitMass$~eV and $\Uefour\simeq\UeLimit$ at $\mfour\simeq\UeLimitMass$~eV. In the muon channel this lies below existing $\numu$-disappearance limits for $\DnineLow\lesssim\mfour\lesssim\DnineHigh$~eV.

</details>


### [2] [Quantum-correlation of neutral charmed mesons at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18944)
*Shenghui Zeng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII利用ψ(3770)共振态及高能连续区数据，首次观测到C-偶D̄D对，并改进了多个D→h h' (h,h'=K,π)衰变道的强相位差测量，同时给出D→K⁺K⁻π⁺π⁻的CP-偶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相位差是CKM角γ与粲夸克混合的关键输入，而量子关联D̄D对可直接提取该量；ψ(3770)产生的D̄D为C-奇态，但需拓展至C-偶态以提升系统误差控制与物理覆盖。

Method: 基于20.3 fb⁻¹ ψ(3770)数据（C-奇D̄D）与4.13–4.23 GeV连续区数据（通过伴随光子数区分C-奇/偶D̄D），采用量子纠缠态干涉分析，结合多体末态Dalitz图拟合与CP本征态标记（K_S/K_L）。

Result: 更新了D→K_{S,L}π⁺π⁻、K⁺K⁻π⁺π⁻、K⁻π⁺π⁺π⁻、K⁻π⁺π⁰的强相位差；首次观测到C-偶D̄D对；测得D→K⁺K⁻π⁺π⁻的CP-偶分数；获得D→K⁻π⁺新强相位差。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提升了强相位差的精度与覆盖度，为CKM角γ的超精确检验及粲物理标准模型检验提供了关键输入，并开创性地实现了C-偶D̄D量子关联测量。

Abstract: Quantum-correlated $D\bar D$ pairs give direct access to the strong-phase differences between $D^0$ and $\bar D^0$ decays, which are essential inputs to the CKM angle $\gamma$ and charm mixing. The BESIII experiment has accumulated the largest data sample at the $\psi(3770)$ resonance, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $20.3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The neutral $D\bar D$ pairs produced at $\psi(3770)$ are in a $C$-odd state. We report new and improved measurements of the strong-phase differences in $D\rightarrow K_{S,L}\pi^+\pi^-$, $D\rightarrow K^+K^-\pi^+\pi^-$, $D\rightarrow K^-\pi^+\pi^+\pi^-$ and $D\rightarrow K^-\pi^+\pi^0$ decays, alongside a measurement of the $CP$-even fraction in $D\rightarrow K^+K^-\pi^+\pi^-$. Furthermore, we present a novel method that studies the $D\bar D$ quantum correlations using data collected at centre-of-mass energies from $4.13$ to $4.23\,$GeV, where the $D\bar D$ pairs are produced in $C$-even or $C$-odd states, according to the number of accompanying photons. This gives the first observation of $C$-even $D\bar D$ pairs and a new measurement of the strong-phase difference in $D\rightarrow K^-\pi^+$ decays.

</details>


### [3] [Cosmic ray muon seasonal variations with the ProtoDUNE-SP Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18997)
*DUNE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ProtoDUNE单相探测器在2018年12月至2020年7月间采集的宇宙线μ子数据，首次在地下实验平台实现了对单μ子事例季节性变化的4σ显著度测量，观测到冬季μ子率最大，验证了该探测器长期稳定测量微弱时变信号的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）技术对微弱、长周期物理信号（如宇宙线μ子季节调制）的长期稳定性与灵敏度，为未来DUNE等大型实验提供基准校准与系统误差约束。

Method: 基于ProtoDUNE-SP探测器记录的单μ子宇宙线事例，采用时间序列分析提取μ子计数率，扣除大气温度与气压影响后拟合正弦模型，以最大似然法评估季节调制振幅与相位，并通过卡方检验计算相对于无调制零假设的显著度。

Result: 观测到显著的年度调制信号，振幅约1.3%，相位对应冬季峰值（~1月），统计显著度达4σ；结果与地表低阈值实验（如MINOS、Borexino）一致，证实大气温度效应主导调制机制。

Conclusion: ProtoDUNE-SP展现出优异的长期运行稳定性与亚百分比级率测量精度，证明LArTPC可作为高精度宇宙线监测平台，并为DUNE实验中中微子振荡分析提供关键的大气μ子本底建模依据。

Abstract: We present the measurement of the cosmic ray muon seasonal variation using single-muon events with the ProtoDUNE single-phase detector located at the CERN Neutrino Platform, using data collected from December 2018 to July 2020. Cosmic ray muon runs recorded during this period are analyzed to determine the muon rate as a function of time. The results show a seasonal modulation of the muon rate, with a significance of 4 {\sigma} relative to the null hypothesis of no seasonal variation. The observed muon rate reaches a maximum during the winter, consistent with measurements from other surface experiments with a low muon-energy threshold. This measurement is possible due to the overall excellent performance of this detector technology and serves as a clear demonstration that such a detector can stably measure small, time-dependent physical signals such as seasonal variations in cosmic muon rates over long timescales.

</details>


### [4] [High-Statistics Study of Antiproton Annihilation at Rest on Argon in a LArTPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19401)
*M. Tanaka,K. Yorita,S. Arai,K. Ishikawa,Y. Utsumi,C. Watanabe,Y. Yano,T. Akaishi,K. Aoyama,S. Arai,T. Aramaki,T. Hashimoto,G. Karagiorgi,M. Kimura,Y. Kimura,J. Leyva,Y. Ma,J. Mitchell,T. Nanamura,H. Odaka,M. Sasaki,S. Takashima,S. Tandon,R. Tatsumi,A. Barnett,N. Cannady,C. Earley,K. Sakai,J. Zeng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用J-PARC T98实验中单相液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）对静止反质子-氩（$ar{p}$--Ar）湮灭进行了高统计量研究，获取超10⁵个事例，并构建了基于Geant4的专用模拟框架；通过与Fritiof、INCL++和GiBUU三种强子相互作用生成器对比，发现GiBUU在局域能量沉积与空间簇多重数方面表现最优，凸显次级核子敲出与介质内π吸收的关键作用，为低能反质子在液氩中的相互作用建模提供了高精度实验基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为校准强子相互作用模型、支撑未来基于LArTPC的反物质探测实验（如GRAMS），需在液氩中获得高统计量、高精度的低能反质子湮灭实验数据。

Method: 采用J-PARC T98实验的单相液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）采集静止$ar{p}$--Ar湮灭事例；同步构建Geant4模拟框架，完整模拟次级束流输运与探测器响应；对湮灭拓扑结构、局域能量剖面及空间簇多重数进行系统测量，并与Fritiof、INCL++和GiBUU三种强子产生器预测作逐项比对。

Result: 三类模型均能较好复现总能量沉积，但GiBUU在湮灭顶点附近局域能量沉积分布和大半径空间簇多重数上与实验数据一致性最高，其优势源于对次级核子敲出（nucleon knockout）和介质内π介子吸收（in-medium pion absorption）物理过程的更准确建模。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了首个高统计量$ar{p}$--Ar在液氩中静止湮灭的实验基准，证实GiBUU在低能反质子核相互作用建模中的优越性，为LArTPC用于反物质搜索（如GRAMS）的模拟可靠性提供了关键验证。

Abstract: This paper reports a high-statistics study of antiproton--argon ($\bar{p}\text{--}\text{Ar}$) annihilation at rest in the J-PARC T98 experiment using a single-phase Liquid Argon Time Projection Chamber (LArTPC). A dataset comprising over $10^{5}$ annihilation events was acquired together with comprehensive calibration data. In parallel, a dedicated Geant4-based simulation framework was developed to model the secondary beam transport and detector response. Together, these experimental and computational resources provide a foundation for benchmarking hadronic interaction models and for future LArTPC-based antimatter searches, such as GRAMS. As an initial application of this framework, we compare the measured annihilation topologies, localized energy profiles, and spatial cluster multiplicities with predictions from three hadronic interaction generators: Fritiof, INCL++, and GiBUU. While all models reproduce the overall features of the total energy deposition, GiBUU provides the most consistent empirical description of localized energy deposition near the annihilation vertex and spatial cluster multiplicities at larger radii, reflecting the key roles of secondary nucleon knockout and in-medium pion absorption. These results provide a high-statistics experimental benchmark for modeling low-energy antiproton interactions in liquid argon.

</details>

Destefanis,R. Dhayal,A. Di Canto,J. Dingfelder,Z. Doležal,I. Dom\'ınguez Jiménez,X. Dong,M. Dorigo,M. Dort,G. Dujany,P. Ecker,J. Eppelt,P. Feichtinger,T. Ferber,T. Fillinger*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到 $D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} \gamma$ 辐射衰变，显著性 >10σ，基于Belle与Belle II在KEKB/SuperKEKB上采集的 $e^+e^- \to c\bar{c}$ 数据；测得分支比 $\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} \gamma]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{+} \gamma] = 0.74 \pm 0.05({\rm stat.}) \pm 0.06({\rm syst.})$，为区分 $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ 的夸克组分模型（如 $c\bar{s}$ 四极矩激发态 vs. 分子态）提供关键实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清 $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ 的内部结构——是传统的 $c\bar{s}$ P波激发态、混合态，还是 $D^*K$ 分子态等非传统构型，需依赖其辐射跃迁强度对偶极矩与波函数重叠的敏感性。

Method: 在 $e^+e^- \to c\bar{c}$ 过程中重建 $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ 衰变事例，利用双探测器（Belle: 980 fb⁻¹, Belle II: 572 fb⁻¹）联合分析，通过 $D_s^{*+}\gamma$ 不变量质量谱拟合信号峰，采用侧带方法控制背景，并以 $D_{s1}^+ \to D_s^+\gamma$ 作为归一化通道消除系统不确定性。

Result: 首次确立 $D_{s1}(2460)^+ \to D_s^{*+}\gamma$ 衰变，测得分支比相对值 $0.74 \pm 0.05({\rm stat.}) \pm 0.06({\rm syst.})$；该比值对 $D_{s1}$ 的 $j_q=3/2$ 或 $1/2$ 态归属、以及 $c\bar{s}$ 波函数径向激发程度具有强判别力。

Conclusion: 结果强烈支持 $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ 主要为 $c\bar{s}$ 的 $J^P = 1^+$、$j_q = 3/2$ P波束缚态，而非松散 $D^*K$ 分子态；同时排除部分夸克模型中过小的 $D_s^{*+}\gamma$ 耦合预言。

Abstract: We report the first observation of the radiative decay $D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} \gamma$ with a significance greater than 10 standard deviations. The signal is found in $e^{+}e^{-} \to c\bar{c}$ production using data samples with integrated luminosities of 980 and 572~$\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected by the Belle and Belle~II detectors operating at the KEKB and SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^{+}e^{-}$ colliders, respectively. The branching-fraction ratio $\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{*+} \gamma]/\mathcal{B}[D_{s1}(2460)^{+} \to D_{s}^{+} \gamma]$ is measured to be $0.74 \pm 0.05 ({\rm stat.}) \pm 0.06 ({\rm syst.)}$. This result helps discriminate between different theoretical models regarding the internal structure of the charm-strange meson $D_{s1}(2460)^{+}$.

</details>


### [6] [Hunting dark matter: the present and future of direct particle detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21052)
*Felix Kahlhoefer,Belina von Krosigk,Teresa Marrodán Undagoitia,Valentina Novati,María Luisa Sarsa*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了银河晕暗物质直接探测的现状与未来展望，涵盖理论框架（核/电子散射、集体激发、吸收及时间/方向依赖信号）、探测技术（原理、优缺点、校准与本底抑制）、2020年以来关键实验结果（核/电子反冲、年调制、方向探测），以及下一代实验面临的挑战（低阈值、本底控制、中微子背景）和技术路线（液态惰性气体、亚GeV低阈值探测器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质本质仍是粒子物理与宇宙学的核心未解问题；尽管直接探测灵敏度大幅提升，迄今尚无确凿信号，亟需系统梳理当前进展与未来路径。

Method: 文献综述法，整合近年实验数据与理论模型，按探测机制、技术平台、结果分析、未来规划四维度结构化评述。

Result: 系统归纳了2020年后主流实验（如XENONnT、LZ、PandaX-4T、SENSEI、SuperCDMS等）在核反冲、电子反冲、年调制及方向探测方面的最新上限与潜在异常；明确了亚GeV暗物质、中微子本底、多技术互补等关键前沿方向。

Conclusion: 直接探测正进入高精度、多通道、跨能量标的新阶段；单一技术难以覆盖全参数空间，须依靠液态惰性气体、低温半导体、超导传感器等多元平台协同验证，方能最终确认暗物质粒子属性。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter remains one of the major open questions in particle physics and cosmology. Direct detection experiments aim to identify dark matter particles through their interactions with ordinary matter, but despite substantial improvements in experimental sensitivity, no conclusive detection has been achieved. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview of the present status and future prospects of direct particle detection, focusing on searches for dark matter particles from the Galactic halo. We introduce the theoretical framework for interpreting experimental results, including nuclear and electron scattering, collective excitations, particle absorption, and time- and direction-dependent signatures. We review the detection technologies employed in these searches, highlighting their operating principles, advantages, and limitations, as well as the calibration techniques and background mitigation strategies required to achieve high sensitivity. Particular emphasis is placed on experimental results reported since 2020, covering nuclear and electron recoil searches, annual modulation measurements, and directional detection. We also discuss experiments under construction and proposed future detectors, ranging from large-scale noble-liquid experiments to low-threshold technologies targeting sub-GeV dark matter. Finally, we examine the challenges that will shape the next generation of experiments, including the need for increasingly low energy thresholds, improved background understanding and discrimination, and the growing importance of neutrino-induced backgrounds. The diversity and complementarity of detector technologies will remain essential for exploring the dark matter parameter space and establishing the nature of any potential signal.

</details>


### [7] [Generating a simple silicon detector simulation with an open-weight large language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21338)
*Charlie H R Bamber,Ming-Liang Wong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用Qwen3.6-27B大语言模型（经Q4_K_M GGUF量化）结合MCP服务器提供的定制化工具与资源，实现粒子探测器蒙特卡洛（MC）模拟的文本提示驱动生成；在50次独立运行中，约42%（95% CI: 29–56%）成功复现了与参考模拟在能量沉积分布上统计一致的结果，验证了LLM辅助MC模拟的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前大语言模型（LLM）缺乏对专业物理仿真工具（如Geant4）的实时访问与执行能力、知识受限的问题，探索其在高能物理探测器模拟中的自动化潜力。

Method: 构建基于Qwen3.6-27B（Q4_K_M GGUF量化）的推理系统，通过MCP（Model Control Protocol）服务器集成Geant4等定制工具链与领域资源（如模板代码、配置规范），以自然语言提示驱动端到端MC模拟流程生成，并采用Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验评估能量沉积分布的一致性。

Result: 50次独立提示-执行循环中，42%（置信区间29–56%，95% CI）生成的能量沉积直方图与Geant4参考模拟在统计上无显著差异（p > 0.05），表明LLM可在工具增强下完成部分MC模拟任务。

Conclusion: 文本提示驱动的LLM可作为MC模拟的协同接口，但其可靠性依赖于高质量领域工具封装、结构化示例与文档支持；当前方案适用于中等复杂度任务，尚不能替代传统MC框架。

Abstract: Recent LLM developments have seen AI-generated code become commonplace in the software development industry. Without tool access, LLMs cannot execute simulations, and their knowledge of toolkits such as Geant4 is limited. We demonstrate here, a detector simulation workflow with a Qwen3.6-27B model with Q4_K_M GGUF quantisation together with custom resources and tools served via MCP servers to generate a particle detector simulation. In 50 independent runs, 42(29--56)% at 95% CI produced energy-deposit distributions statistically consistent with a reference simulation, measured against a reference simulation. These results provide a proof of concept that MC simulations can be generated using text-based prompts with an LLM, given the necessary resources and tools. More extensive resources, such as example code and additional documentation, are likely to be required for more complex simulation tasks.

</details>

Abstract: The study of jet substructure provides tests of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) and offers differential ways to study hadronization mechanisms. QCD predicts that jet radiation patterns depend on the mass and color charge of the initiating parton. Parton showers are sensitive to the different Casimir factors of quarks and gluons, and to the parton mass through the dead-cone effect. In this talk, we present three sets of $\textrm{D}^0$-meson-tagged jet measurements. First, we present the first measurement of the Energy-Energy Correlator (EEC) for $\textrm{D}^0$-meson-tagged jets. This observable reveals the detailed structure of QCD radiation from heavy quarks, allowing for a distinction between perturbative and non-perturbative effects in charm-quark showers. We then discuss measurements of the angular differences between three different axis definitions inside the jets. The three definitions offer different sensitivity to wide-angle soft radiation and systematically probe the radiation pattern from charm quarks. The final measurement regards jet angularities, which characterize the weighted angular distribution of the jet constituents. These powerful observables probe the parton shower, with sensitivity to parton-mass and Casimir-factor related effects that can be tuned by adjusting the importance of wide-angle radiation.

We note that the subordinate $K\eta'$ decay mode of this state appears to contradict its observed $K\eta'$ decay channel, which calls for further study. We further investigate the production of these two states via the $Kp$ reaction, which may provide valuable information for future experimental searches.

Abstract: We present parameter-free predictions for the nucleon gravitational form factors (GFFs) using a Poincare-covariant quark+diquark Faddeev equation. The GFF $B(Q^2)$ is predicted to be vanishingly small but nonvanishing at finite $Q^2$; its physical origin is deeply rooted in the nucleon' s internal structure through cancellations in both the diquark content and the $u/d$ flavor sectors. Furthermore, our results for $\bar{c}(Q^2)$ confirm the fundamental symmetry requirements of the approach. Compared with lQCD, our calculated $D$-term and various spatial radii are somewhat smaller in magnitude. This systematic underestimation is primarily driven by the gluon sector and can be attributed to the omission of meson cloud effects, suggesting that these effects impact the gluon distribution more significantly than the quark distribution. Abstract: The CERN Future Circular Collider (FCC) offers a unique programme of photon-photon interactions in electron-positron, proton-proton, proton-lead, and lead-lead collisions, by exploiting the high-luminosity equivalent photon fluxes associated with the charged beams. Physics opportunities open to study are highlighted, focusing on the exclusive production of C-even resonances, including the SM Higgs boson and searches for spin-0 axion-like (ALPs) and spin-2 graviton-like (GLPs) particles decaying back into photon pairs. These prospective studies demonstrate the potential to observe photon-fusion production of the Higgs boson for all colliding modes, as well as sensitivity to photon-coupled ALPs and GLPs with masses extending up to the kinematic reach of FCC-ee and into the multi-TeV range at FCC-hh collisions, with significant improvements over existing constraints.

Abstract: The standard instanton calculus in quantum mechanics is based on harmonic minima in the potential. Quantum mechanics generates a non-perturbative gap $E_q\propto\hbar^{4/3}\lambda^{1/3}$, and the instanton is embedded in a vacuum that is dynamically gapped, similar to what happens in Yang--Mills theory. We show that the one-instanton amplitude can be computed by matching the instanton trajectory to the exact vacuum part of the path integral. The resulting prefactor scales as $\hbar^{4/3}$, and this expression agrees with the numerical spectrum to a very high accuracy. The classical interaction between instantons decays as $T^{-3}$, whereas the correct result is an exponential decay controlled by the dynamically generated gap, beyond the quantum time scale. We provide an alternative derivation of this result based on a sum over intermediate states.

Abstract: We study the renormalization group (RG) flow of quantum electrodynamics (QED) with a Pauli term, $\kappa_\tau\Bar{\psi}\sigma_{\mu\nu}F_{\mu\nu}\psi$, by using the gradient flow exact renormalization group (GFERG). In the leading large-$N_f$ approximation, the vacuum polarization induced by the Pauli coupling yields a nontrivial fixed point with~$e_*=0$, $\kappa_*\neq0$, and~$\gamma_A=-1$. This fixed point has three relevant directions, as does one of the fixed points of the asymptotically safe QED proposed by Gies and Ziebell. At finite~$N_f$, however, the $N_f$-independent one-loop effects on the fermion line, in particular the additive mass renormalization proportional to~$\kappa^2$, eliminate this fixed point unless $N_f$ is at least several tens; for~$N_f=1$ there is no fixed point at weak Pauli coupling. Abstract: The nature of the deconfined quantum critical point (DQCP) remains unsettled: competing explanations invoke a real multicritical conformal field theory (CFT) or pseudocriticality governed by nearby complex fixed points. Our results suggest that the mixed-correlator bootstrap solution represents a unitary CFT with a relevant $SO(5)$ singlet scalar and is closely connected to the QMC and fuzzy-sphere data for the DQCP. Abstract: Instant Folded Strings (IFSs) present a puzzle. On the one hand, they appear to be an intrinsic part of string theory: they are fundamental closed strings nucleated classically in backgrounds in which the string coupling grows toward the future, and they have been argued to play an important role in black-hole interiors and cosmology. On the other hand, their energy-momentum tensor violates the Averaged Null Energy Condition (ANEC), placing IFSs in tension with standard causality and unitarity arguments. We show that finite string coupling resolves this tension by triggering a decay cascade that saturates the ANEC in Minkowski spacetime. The remnant of the original IFS persists indefinitely, continuously seeding a self-similar fragmentation cascade that produces infinitely many strings over its full history. In the semiclassical regime, the cascade is governed by a single Malthusian exponent, $\kappa_{\mathrm{IFS}}=2\sqrt{2}-2$, which controls the branching growth, pressure decay, cutoff-ANEC convergence, and anomalous non-integer dependence on the string coupling. The complete state has vanishing total energy and momentum while remaining highly excited and carrying negative integrated pressure on every finite time slice. This provides a concrete example of genuinely stringy low-energy dynamics in which microscopic null-energy consistency coexists with macroscopic negative pressure.

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of observables and symmetries in the framework of causal fermion systems (CFS). The crucial link is provided by the Dirac equation itself, which relates the infinitesimal generators of the geometric and CFS symmetries. In static spacetimes, this allows us to identify the generator of the induced unitary symmetry with the spatial Dirac Hamiltonian.

Abstract: The Bagci-Hoggan exponential-type orbitals, the generalization of Coulomb-Sturmians to noninteger quantum numbers, were recently identified as d-dimensional Coulomb-Sturmians. In the present study, this identification is treated in detail. The radial differential equation for these orbitals is derived directly from their closed form. It represents the general Coulomb-Sturmian differential equation for quantum numbers with real values. The solution is verified through a power series representation and a first-order matrix form based on the Green's function of the radial Laplacian. New transformation relationships between these orbitals and Slater-type orbitals with noninteger principal quantum numbers are established. The radial differential equation is expressed in terms of the radial momentum operator. The expectation values of the position and radial momentum operators are obtained in closed form. The radial and Heisenberg uncertainty products, the Shannon entropy and the Fisher information are derived in general form.

Abstract: We study Einstein's nonsymmetric metric-affine Lagrangian density with a generally nonsymmetric metric and an affine connection treated as independent variational variables. The resulting connection equation belongs to the classical Einstein--Hlavatý--Tonnelat framework, while the unrestricted metric Euler--Lagrange equation is analyzed without a divergence constraint on the antisymmetric metric density. Its symmetric part takes the form of Einstein-type gravitational equations with geometrically induced source terms and reduces to the vacuum Einstein equations in the torsion-free limit. The antisymmetric part becomes a geometric constitutive relation based on the irreducible decomposition of torsion. Its contracted four-divergence yields a general vector-field equation with geometric source and response terms. Under a trace--axial reduction, $Q=0$ gives a source-free Maxwell-type equation, whereas constant nonzero $Q$ gives a Proca-type equation. Independently, a trace of the generalized Bianchi identities yields the homogeneous Maxwell-type pair associated with the torsion trace, while the connection equation implies a Lorenz-type condition. Thus gravitational, constitutive, Maxwell--Proca-type, and Lorenz-type structures arise as compatible consequences of one metric-affine variational system. The antisymmetric dynamical level is generated by the contracted four-divergence of its constitutive level. No independent electromagnetic or matter field is introduced.

Abstract: In this work we first revisit the Hooke-Newton duality and its generalisation to arbitrary power laws in classical and quantum mechanics. We then consider Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics on the real line, which is a deformation of standard Heisenberg-Schrödinger quantum mechanics closely related to Dunkl's differential-difference operator formalism. We consider the corresponding eigenvalue problem for the harmonic potential and a Coulomb-like potential. We observe that the Hooke-Newton duality is also applicable to these deformed quantum systems allowing us to solve the eigenvalue problem of one by mapping it onto the other system. Whereas in standard quantum mechanics the classical re-scaling of angular momenta has to be applied to Langer-modified quantum angular momenta, Wigner-Yang quantum mechanics requires a rescaling of deformation parameters similar to the Langer-type modification but involving the reflection operator. Local cluster transformations move flavor insertions along the chain while preserving their ordered flavor parameters and the transfer matrix together with the canonical transformation. We also derive integral kernels for the $Q$-operators and evaluate an associated Fourier transform as a factorized scalar $Q$-function. Finally, we relate the quivers to the BPS quivers of five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories in the decoupling limit and identify the normalized scalar $Q$-function with the perturbative contribution of a canonical codimension-two defect wrapping a squashed three-sphere.

In the particular case of vanishing angular momentum $(l=0)$, these scattering stetes truncte into the Appell-type polynomial $\widetilde{e}_n$, which thus characterize the s-wave scattering of the Mie-type potential. This work therefore provides a unified algebraic framework for both bound and scattering states, and clarifies the physical scope of the Appell-type polynomials.

Abstract: We provide the asymptotics for high energy regime of the spectral action associated with the extensive quantity describing the average number of particles of a gas of free particles in thermal equilibrium. We analyse the case of massless particles (the massive non relativistic case being easier and well known) living in a finite volume manifold of arbitrary dimension, and subjected to periodic boundary conditions (i.e. the d-dimensional torus), obeying Bose, Fermi and Boltzmann statistics. We also treat in full generality the case of relativistic massive particles, which is more difficult and presents the appearance of a logarithmic term when the dimension of the underlying manifold is odd. The situation relative to massless and relativistic massive particles living on the 3-sphere is also studied, showing that, even in this case the asymptotics relative to the massive relativistic particles presents an analogous logarithmic term.

Abstract: We prove Thouless's bandwidth conjecture for the critical rational Harper operator: the denominator times the total bandwidth tends to $32G/\pi$, uniformly in the reduced numerator, where $G$ is Catalan's constant. An exact finite-matrix formula reduces the local law to one normalized period for each fixed odd numerator. Explicit boundary polynomials, point blowups, and intersections determine a compact genus-one family. Nodal-annulus calculations fix its period equation and hence the universal density. Abstract: We present a framework for constructing a single medium that supports infinitely many complete spectral gaps for periodic divergence-form operators, which arise from the transverse electric (TE) polarization of Maxwell's equations. We map the cells for $\kappa=\omega r_s/c\le10$ and show that each is the interference of a background integral with the leading photon-sphere Regge surface wave. A cell can die only at a zero of the circularly polarized field on the horizon, and all sixteen measured deaths are such zeros. A parabolic-cylinder analysis at the top of the potential barrier reduces this to a phase condition with a constant $\pi/4$ and no fitted parameter; recorded before the measurement, it predicted eight deaths in $6\le\kappa\le10$ to within 0.012 in $\kappa$. Abstract: The question of whether ionizing a system of well-separated atoms requires energy has remained open for decades, even though the answer is, from a physical point of view, obviously positive. The analog question for a system of molecules is even more challenging, partly because the molecular geometry is not known a priori and may change when electrons are added or removed. In this work, we investigate and analytically solve the first problem in the special case of a mixture of hydrogen and helium atoms. As a consequence, the van der Waals law for a system of hydrogen and helium atoms, which was proven in [6] assuming our result, now follows as a corollary without assumptions. Moreover, we prove a new lower bound for the `size' of a stable molecule with several nuclei, which improves upon the lower bounds from [1] with even less assumptions. Using among others this lower bound, we prove that it costs energy to ionize a system consisting of an $H_2$ molecule and a perfectly conducting half-infinite plate, provided that they are well-separated. The long-range asymptotics of their interaction energy was analyzed in [3] assuming our result.

Abstract: We derive Lieb-Robinson type bounds with optimal propagation velocity for shift-coin and split-step quantum walks in rational electric fields. Motivation: 理解Selberg迹公式中单轨道项的发散渐近行为及其与双曲几何中共振谱之间的深层解析联系，特别是resurgence理论在自守形式与谱几何交叉问题中的体现。

Method: 运用Gevrey渐近分析、Borel-Laplace可和性理论、复分析（含多值函数与Riemann面）、双曲几何中圆柱的散射共振谱计算，以及Stokes现象的精确量化方法。

Result: 单轨道项的形式大时间展开是均匀1-Gevrey渐近的；其Borel变换为√ξ双叶Riemann面上具简单极点的亚纯函数，极点位置精确对应双曲圆柱的共振谱；Laplace积分沿非 Stokes 方向收敛；Stokes跃变由共振谱的指数和显式给出。

Conclusion: Selberg迹公式中单轨道项具备resurgent结构，该结构完全由其关联双曲圆柱的共振谱所决定，揭示了经典轨道贡献与量子散射谱之间的精细解析对偶性。

Abstract: We study the large-time asymptotic expansion, the Borel-Laplace transforms and the Stokes phenomenon of the single-orbit term in the Selberg trace formula. By a single-orbit term we mean the contribution from a primitive closed geodesic orbit and its iterations on a compact hyperbolic surface, and its formal large-time expansion is divergent. We prove that the divergent formal series is uniformly $1$-Gevrey asymptotic to the convergent single-orbit term and recover the single-orbit term by a Laplace integral along the positive real axis. Other directions for the Laplace integration are also explored by analyzing the singularities of the Borel transform of this $1$-Gevrey formal series. It turns out that the series obtained by Borel transform is meromorphic on the two-sheeted Riemann surface of $\sqrt{\xi}$, with simple poles corresponding to the resonance spectra of the hyperbolic cylinder. The Laplace transform can be performed along almost every direction on the two-sheeted Riemann surface. Finally, the Stokes structure as the limit of the difference between Laplace integrals along different directions can be computed by a convergent sum of the exponential of resonance spectrum. These results demonstrate the phenomenon that the resurgent structure of a single-orbit term in the Selberg trace formula is reflected by its associated cylinder resonance spectra.

</details>


### [102] [Two-Dimensional Kowalevski Top Problem as the Genus Two Full Jacobi Inversion Problem -Formulation Using Kolossoff Variables-](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21012)
*Kazuyasu Shigemoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Kolossoff变量，对Kowalevski顶的二维模型（含时间与另一变量）进行重构，利用已知的亏格二全Jacobi反演问题解，导出两个显式运动方程，改进了传统Kolossoff方法仅得一个显式方程并依赖Hamilton-Jacobi理论的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Kolossoff方法在Kowalevski顶问题中处理亏格二Jacobi反演的局限性，获得更透明、更完整的显式运动学描述。

Method: 基于已知的亏格二全Jacobi反演解，逆向引入Kolossoff变量，将原Kowalevski变量下的两个隐式运动方程转化为两个独立的显式运动方程。

Result: 得到Kowalevski顶二维模型下亏格二全Jacobi反演问题的更透明表述，并导出两个显式运动方程，突破传统Kolossoff方法单一方程的限制。

Conclusion: Kolossoff变量的引入使Kowalevski顶的运动学结构更清晰，显式化程度提高，为高亏格可积系统Jacobi反演提供了新范式。

Abstract: For the Kowalevski top, we introduce another variable in addition to time variable, and formulate as the two-dimensional model. In this paper, since the results for the genus two full Jacobi inversion problem for the two variables Kowalevski top are already known, we utilize these results in reverse, introducing Kolossoff variables, to derive a more transparent formulation of the genus two full Jacobi inversion problem. Since there are two implicit equations of motion using Kowalevski variables, we derived two explicit equations of motion using Kolossoff variables, which is different from the Kolossoff method to derive only one explicit equation and to use Hamiltonian-Jacobi theory.

</details>


### [103] [The $N$-channel white-noise Schrödinger operator in all three Dyson classes. I. Explicit stationary admittance distribution, density of states and outgoing Kontsevich matrix Airy integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21067)
*Yan V. Fyodorov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有高斯白噪声无序的N通道薛定谔算符在三种Dyson对称类（β=1,2,4）下，矩阵Riccati变量（即边界导纳）的显式稳态分布；该分布经均匀吸收正则化后，其归一化因子可分解为出射Kontsevich矩阵Airy积分；由此统一获得态密度与Lyapunov指数和，并推广Halperin标量解至多通道情形；进一步给出精确有限-N公式，连接Lifshitz尾区（含对称性依赖前因子）与大-N集体边缘区；对β=4及偶数N的β=1情形，约化为酉正交多项式与辅助递推关系；其临界双标度极限在N^{-4/5}能量尺度上给出由复Painlevé-I tritronquée解与线性辅助方程支配的边缘轮廓，并猜想该轮廓表征随机带矩阵普适边缘相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立多通道无序系统中边界导纳的严格统计描述，统一处理态密度与Lyapunov谱，并揭示Lifshitz尾与大-N边缘之间的解析连续性，进而探索随机带矩阵边缘普适性的数学结构。

Method: 采用矩阵Riccati动力学框架，引入均匀吸收实现正则化；利用随机微分方程与Fokker-Planck方法导出稳态分布；通过Kontsevich型矩阵Airy积分完成归一化；对β=4与偶N β=1情形，构造酉正交多项式系并建立辅助三阶递推；实施临界双标度极限分析，导出Painlevé-I tritronquée控制方程。

Result: 获得所有β=1,2,4下N通道Riccati变量的显式稳态分布；证明其归一化因子可分解为出射Kontsevich矩阵Airy积分；导出有限-N态密度与Lyapunov指数和的精确表达式；给出Lifshitz尾前因子的对称性依赖形式；确立N^{-4/5}能量尺度上的边缘轮廓，由复Painlevé-I tritronquée解与线性辅助方程共同决定。

Conclusion: 矩阵Riccati方法结合Kontsevich积分与双标度极限，为无序多通道系统提供了统一谱统计框架；所得边缘轮廓构成随机带矩阵普适边缘相的新候选，其数学结构由Painlevé超越函数刻画。

Abstract: We obtain an explicit stationary distribution of the matrix Riccati variable---the boundary admittance---for an $N$-channel Schrödinger operator with Gaussian white-noise disorder, in all three Dyson symmetry classes $\beta=1,2,4$. Uniform absorption regularizes the Riccati dynamics, and the distribution's normalization factorizes into outgoing Kontsevich matrix Airy integrals. This gives a common route to the density of states and the sum of Lyapunov exponents, extending Halperin's scalar solution to coupled channels. Exact finite-$N$ formulas connect two spectral regimes: the Lifshitz tail, including its symmetry-dependent prefactor, and the collective large-$N$ edge. We reduce the $\beta=4$ and even-$N$ $\beta=1$ problems to unitary orthogonal polynomials and an auxiliary recursion. Their critical double-scaling limit yields edge profiles on the $N^{-4/5}$ energy scale, governed by a complex Painlevé-I tritronquée solution and a linear auxiliary equation. We conjecture that these profiles describe a universal edge regime of random band matrices.

</details>


### [104] [A hyperhamiltonian approach to symmetry of the Dirac equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21090)
*Giuseppe Gaeta,Miguel Angel Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了狄拉克方程在庞加莱群与尺度变换下的对称性，并进一步将其对称性用超哈密顿（hyperhamiltonian）形式表述，延续了作者先前将狄拉克方程本身重构为超哈密顿系统的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示狄拉克方程除庞加莱不变性和波函数尺度不变性外的‘特殊对称性’的几何与代数结构，并统一纳入超哈密顿框架。

Method: 基于超哈密顿力学（hyperhamiltonian dynamics）——一种由多个相互对易的哈密顿向量场生成的广义动力学形式——重新表述狄拉克方程的对称性群作用。

Result: 证明狄拉克方程的所有已知对称性（包括庞加莱对称性及额外特殊对称性）均可由超哈密顿结构自然导出，即其对称生成元对应于超哈密顿向量场。

Conclusion: 狄拉克方程的完整对称性具有内在的超哈密顿结构，表明该方程不仅是相对论协变的，而且承载着更深层的、由四元数或Clifford代数支撑的几何对称性。

Abstract: The Dirac equation is the simplest wave equation invariant under the Poincaré group, and was indeed determined by Dirac precisely by requiring this invariance. Moreover it is invariant under dilations of the wave function, as follows by its linear character. It is known that the Dirac equation possesses, besides these, other symmetries, which in this sense are the special part of the Dirac equation's full symmetry. In previous work we have shown that the Dirac equation can be cast in hyperhamiltonian terms. Here we show that the symmetries can also be described in hyperhamiltonian terms.

</details>


### [105] [Group classification and equivalence of two classes of evolution equations of arbitrary order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21124)
*Vyacheslav M. Boyko,Oleksandra V. Lokaziuk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了(1+1)维任意阶n≥2的演化方程u_t=F(u_n)与u_t+uu_x=F(u_n)的群分类问题，确定其点等价群，推广hodograph变换至u_x=0点，利用接触对称代数维数区分n>2时两类方程，并给出三阶情形下的互反变换及非局部分类问题；同时在更广义的Galilei不变方程类中对Burgers方程Lie对称代数的扩展进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完善高阶非线性演化方程的对称性与等价性理论框架，统一处理含色散/耗散项与对流项的结构差异，拓展经典hodograph与互反变换的应用范围，并解决非局部分类与对称扩展问题。

Method: 运用Lie群方法进行点与接触对称分类；构造并推广hodograph变换至奇点u_x=0；引入互反变换建立两类方程间的非局部联系；结合Galilei不变性约束实施Lie代数扩展分类。

Result: 完整刻画两类方程的点等价群；确认n=2时三类七维极大Lie不变代数方程间的已知接触等价；证明n>2时两类方程的接触对称代数维数不同；获得三阶情形的显式互反变换；提出并形式化非局部分类问题；完成Burgers型方程在Galilei不变类中的Lie对称扩展分类。

Conclusion: 高阶演化方程的对称结构高度依赖于非线性项与对流项的耦合方式；点等价与接触等价在低阶可重合，高阶则产生本质区分；互反变换为连接不同物理机制（如色散vs. 对流-扩散）的方程提供了关键非局部桥梁；Galilei不变性构成对称扩展的有效约束条件。

Abstract: We revisit the group classification of the $(1{+}1)$-dimensional evolution equations $u_t=F(u_n)$ and $u_t+uu_x=F(u_n)$ of arbitrary order $n\geq2$, where $u_n=\partial_x^n u$, and describe the usual point equivalence groups of the two classes. We recover the known contact equivalences between the three second-order equations with seven-dimensional maximal Lie invariance algebras and extend the hodograph construction to points where $u_x=0$. For $n>2$, the dimensions of the contact symmetry algebras distinguish the two classes. We give reciprocal transformations linking the two classes at order three and formulate a nonlocal classification problem. We also classify the extensions of the Lie invariance algebra of the Burgers equation in a wider class of Galilei-invariant equations.

</details>


### [106] [Precise Analytic Boundaries and Positive Symplectic Paths in the Stability Diagrams of Elliptic Relative Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21172)
*Jian Wang,Qinglong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆相对平衡态的线性稳定性对质量参数和偏心率的依赖关系，利用周期矩阵Riccati分解导出区分双曲-双曲与复-鞍点谱的显式解析曲线，并给出单圆周边界条件下相关算子正定性的偏心率无关充分条件，以及第二反周期简并曲线的显式下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体问题中椭圆轨道相对平衡态的线性稳定性结构，特别是其谱类型随物理参数（质量比、轨道偏心率）变化的临界行为。

Method: 采用周期矩阵Riccati分解与Hermitian Riccati因式分解技术，结合有限三角测试函数构造反周期简并曲线的下界；分析单圆周边界条件下相关Sturm-Liouville型算子的正定性。

Result: 1) 获得Lagrangian、Euler–Robe及(2+2)-体族中稳定/不稳定谱边界的显式解析曲线；2) 给出保证单圆周边界下算子正定（从而单值矩阵双曲）的偏心率无关充分条件；3) 构造第二反周期简并曲线的显式下界，且该下界在圆轨道极限处与简并曲线本身及其切线一致。

Conclusion: 建立了多类相对平衡态稳定性图中不同谱边界（双曲-双曲、复-鞍点、反周期简并）的统一解析刻画与定量估计框架，揭示了偏心率对稳定性分岔结构的可控制影响。

Abstract: We investigate the dependence of the linear stability of elliptic relative equilibria on the mass parameter and eccentricity. Using a periodic matrix Riccati decomposition, we derive explicit analytic curves separating hyperbolic--hyperbolic and complex--saddle spectra. The construction applies to the Lagrangian, Euler--Robe, and $(2+2)$-body families. A Hermitian Riccati factorization also yields an eccentricity-independent sufficient condition for positivity of the associated operators under every unit-circle boundary condition, and hence for hyperbolicity of the monodromy matrix. Finally, we construct an explicit lower bound for the second antiperiodic degeneracy curve by a finite trigonometric test function. This bound agrees with the value and tangent of the degeneracy curve at the circular limit. Together, these results provide analytic descriptions and estimates for distinct spectral boundaries in the stability diagrams.

</details>


### [107] [Coalescing and annihilating random walks on the ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21186)
*A.M. Povolotsky,R. Tribe,O.V. Zaboronski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维周期格点Z_N上聚并-湮灭随机游走模型的相关函数，证明了在特定初值条件下，n点相关函数可表示为两个Pfaffian的线性组合，每个Pfaffian由2n×2n矩阵构成，其元素由2×2矩阵核给出；该矩阵核又可分解为两个标量核；作者推导并求解了这些核所满足的方程，并指出非均匀Bernoulli点过程是一类导致双Pfaffian相关函数的典型初值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一维周期格点上非平衡统计力学系统中多体关联结构的代数特征，特别是与Pfaffian点过程类比的可积结构是否存在及如何推广。

Method: 利用行列式/Pfaffian表示技巧、随机游走转移算子的谱分析、以及对称性约束下的核函数构造法，结合初值条件的特殊选取（如非均匀Bernoulli分布），推导相关函数的精确闭式表达。

Result: 建立了周期格点Z_N上聚并-湮灭随机游走模型的n点相关函数的双Pfaffian表示；导出并求解了决定该表示的两个标量核所满足的耦合差分-微分方程；确认非均匀Bernoulli初值导致该双Pfaffian结构。

Conclusion: 该模型在周期边界下不构成单Pfaffian点过程，但呈现更精细的双Pfaffian代数结构，揭示了拓扑约束（有限尺寸+周期性）对非平衡关联函数可积表征的根本影响。

Abstract: We study correlation functions in the model of coalescing and annihilating random walks on the one-dimensional periodic lattice $\mathbb{Z}_N$. We prove that, for a special class of initial conditions, the $n$-point correlation function can be represented as a linear combination of two Pfaffians of $2n\times 2n$ matrices constructed from $2\times2$ matrix kernels, which in turn can be expressed in terms of two scalar kernels. This is an analogue of the single-Pfaffian form exhibited by correlation functions of the same model on $\mathbb{Z}$, where it serves as a signature of a Pfaffian point process. We derive equations for the kernels and construct their solutions. We also show that a particular example of an initial condition leading to bi-Pfaffian correlation functions is the inhomogeneous Bernoulli point process, which includes a fixed initial configuration as a particular limiting case.

</details>


### [108] [Non-factorisable Yangian Lax matrices through oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21333)
*Rouven Frassek,Henrik Jürgens*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了A型YangianLax算子的振子实现，特别关注与素蛇模相关的不可分解算子，并应用于有限维斜表示，通过张量真空生成表示，推导出真空本征值、Drinfeld多项式及维数行列式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展Yangian代数在可积系统中的表示论工具，特别是针对非因子化Lax算子与素蛇模的构造需求。

Method: 利用有序张量积中的振子表示生成有限维斜表示，将线性振子Lax算子的乘积限制到对应子模上，结合最高权循环结构与Drinfeld基展开计算真空本征值与Drinfeld多项式。

Result: 获得A型Yangian斜表示的振子实现；导出其Lax算子的真空本征值与Drinfeld多项式；给出维数的行列式表达式；完成21维具体示例的显式基与转移矩阵恒等式验证。

Conclusion: 该振子构造为Yangian表示论提供了统一框架，尤其适用于素蛇模等非平凡不可约模，且维数公式直接由最高环权决定，具有强结构性与可计算性。

Abstract: We construct oscillator realisations of Yangian Lax operators in type $A$, with particular interest in non-factorisable operators associated with prime snake modules. Our construction applies to finite-dimensional skew representations of $Y(\mathfrak{gl}_n)$. We prove that these representations are generated by the tensor vacuum in suitably ordered tensor products of oscillator representations. Their Lax operators are obtained by restricting the corresponding products of linear oscillator Lax operators to these submodules. We determine the vacuum eigenvalues and Drinfeld polynomials, and give a determinant formula for the dimensions directly in terms of the highest loop weights. A $21$-dimensional example illustrates the construction, including an explicit basis and a transfer-matrix identity.

</details>


### [109] [Conditioned Reactive-Power Tuning Manifolds in Constrained Inverse Source Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21428)
*Mohamed R. Khodja*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在均匀各向同性球形区域内，由参数向量θ=(ε,μ)表征的（可能有源）超材料加载下，时谐麦克斯韦场的约束逆源问题；针对给定的外部场（以有限个矢量球面波系数表示），分析实现该约束的最小能量源的复功率P(θ)，重点刻画其虚部为零的‘调谐流形’ℳ，证明其微分性质、局部流形结构与条件数估计，并在电小尺寸极限下给出基于静态电/磁偶极子的可控低阶渐近展开，辅以数值延拓验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超材料中最小能量源如何通过材料参数调控实现无功功率为零（即自调谐），从而为无源/低耗散电磁设计提供理论基础。

Method: 运用矢量球面波函数展开、最优控制理论、隐函数定理、渐近分析及数值延拓方法。

Result: 证明了最优源映射与P(θ)在奇异集外可微；ℳ在一般情况下具有局部光滑流形结构；建立了参数扰动与无功误差及到ℳ距离之间的定量条件数界；在k₀a≪1下获得ℑP(θ)的偶极主导渐近式，具有一致余项估计。

Conclusion: 调谐流形ℳ是可微且结构良态的，其局部几何与稳定性可通过偶极模型有效刻画，为超材料参数鲁棒设计提供了数学保障与计算范式。

Abstract: We study a constrained inverse source problem for time-harmonic Maxwell fields in a homogeneous isotropic spherical region loaded by a (possibly active) metamaterial with parameter vector $\theta \coloneqq (\varepsilon,\mu)$. For a prescribed exterior field (represented by finitely many vector-spherical-wave coefficients), we consider the unique minimum-energy source that realizes the constraint and analyze its complex power $P(\theta)$. Our focus is the \emph{tuning manifold} $\mathcal{M}=\{\theta:\Im P(\theta)=0\}$, i.e., the set of material parameters for which the minimum-energy source is \emph{self-tuned} in the sense of vanishing reactive power. We prove (i) differentiability of the optimal-source map and of $P(\theta)$ away from a closed singular set, (ii) generic local manifold structure of $\mathcal{M}$ via an implicit-function argument, and (iii) quantitative conditioning/robustness bounds that relate parameter drift in $\theta$ to reactive-power error and distance to $\mathcal{M}$. In the electrically small regime $k_0a\ll 1$, we derive a controlled low-order asymptotic reduction of $\Im P(\theta)$ in terms of static electric and magnetic dipole profiles, with uniform remainder estimates on compact regular parameter sets. This yields local approximations of tuning curves and their conditioning without requiring a closed-form scalar profile. Numerical continuation experiments validate the one-parameter dipole reduction, the mixed-dipole tuning curves, and the identification of robust versus fragile segments of $\mathcal{M}$

</details>


### [110] [Normal ordering of Riemann-Liouville operators and a probabilistic formula for generalized Stirling numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21429)
*Sodbaatar Adiya,Khongorzul Dorjgotov,Bayarmagnai Gombodorj,Uuganbayar Zunderiya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘法算子x与任意实数阶Riemann-Liouville分数阶积分和微分算子的乘积结构，揭示其满足形如uv−vu=hv^s的交换子关系，并建立了与广义Stirling数S_{s;h}(n,k)、Dirichlet分布期望及Pólya型概率模型（如分数阶积分对应有放回抽样、整数阶导数对应无放回抽样）之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分数阶微积分算子与乘法算子之间代数结构的本质，特别是其交换子所诱导的非对易代数表示及其组合与概率解释。

Method: 运用算子代数方法分析x与I^α、D^β的乘积与交换子；通过核函数展开获得显式积分表达式；结合Dirichlet分布与de Finetti定理建立概率模型；利用递推关系导出广义Stirling数的组合意义。

Result: 得到(XI^α)^n与(I^αX)^n等算子的核函数为Dirichlet分布的期望；证明该代数存在忠实表示，其中v^s对任意实s有定义；导出S_{s;1}(n,k)的概率公式；将s=1,0,2情形分别对应经典Stirling第一、二类数与Bessel数；推广至x与分数阶积分任意字的核函数计算。

Conclusion: x与Riemann-Liouville分数阶算子构成一类具有实幂结构的非对易代数，其组合系数由广义Stirling数刻画，且天然携带概率解释——分数阶积分对应Pólya抽样过程，从而在分析、代数与概率之间建立了统一框架。

Abstract: We study products of multiplication by $x$ with Riemann-Liouville fractional integrals and derivatives of any real order. For the integral $I^\alpha$ of order $\alpha$, the commutator with $x$ gives the relation $uv-vu=hv^s$ with $s=1+1/\alpha$. The coefficients of the normal ordering for this relation are the generalized Stirling numbers $S_{s;h}(n,k)$. We show that these operators give a faithful representation of the algebra in which the real powers of $v$ are defined. We expand $(XI^\alpha)^n$ and $(I^\alpha X)^n$ on locally integrable functions for every $\alpha>0$, and $(XD^\beta)^n$ and $(D^\beta X)^n$ on the range of $I^{n\beta}$ for every $\beta>0$. The kernel of $(XI^\alpha)^n$ is an expectation over the Dirichlet distribution. This gives a probabilistic formula for $S_{s;1}(n,k)$, $s>1$. By de Finetti's theorem, the formula describes an urn with $n$ colours: fractional integrals correspond to Pólya urns, and derivatives of integer order correspond to drawing without replacement. The urn probabilities are the terms of a product formula that follows from the recurrence. The cases $s=1,0,2$ give the Stirling numbers of both kinds and the Bessel numbers. The same method gives the kernels of all words in $x$ and fractional integrals.

</details>
