Conclusion: mLQC-I中原始扰动在微扰层面自洽，且初态选择显著影响非高斯性形态；尽管两点函数修正微弱，点态bispectrum可极大，但因CMB观测中固有相位平均，实际可观测的f_NL受限；该结果支持mLQC-I作为可行的早期宇宙量子引力候选模型，同时强调需谨慎解读原初非高斯性的观测约束。

Abstract: We investigate primordial non-Gaussianity and the perturbative consistency of scalar perturbations in modified loop quantum cosmology I (mLQC-I) within the hybrid approach. We construct the cubic interaction Hamiltonian in the canonical representation of the quadratic theory and derive an exact finite-momentum in-in expression for the tree-level bispectrum. We consider two initial-state prescriptions: a Gaussian state specified at the bounce and the de Sitter vacuum specified at a remote contracting surface. We assess the retained nonlinear-conversion correction through $\Xi=|\Delta P_R/P_R^{(0)}|$. For the bounce state, a conservative, cancellation-independent, finite-window absolute-integrand estimate is below $6\times10^{-6}$ at all five sampled momenta. For the remote de Sitter state, the signed correction is dominated by cancellations and decreases from $\Xi\simeq9.9\times10^{-2}$ at one tenth of the bounce-curvature scale to $\Xi\simeq2.3\times10^{-4}$ at that scale. Although the retained two-point corrections remain below unity, the bispectrum can have large pointwise amplitudes: the bounce state yields an equilateral $f_{\rm NL}$ with root-mean-square amplitude over oscillation phase of order $10^3$--$10^4$, while the remote state can yield much larger values. The dominant rapid oscillation is organized approximately by $k_t=k_1+k_2+k_3$, with $\Delta k_t\simeq5.7\times10^{-4}m_{\rm P}$, while the triangle-shape envelope varies slowly. For the illustrative bounce-to-present mapping adopted here, CMB-projection diagnostics indicate substantial phase averaging. Thus, large pointwise primordial non-Gaussianity can coexist with a small retained correction to the two-point function.

</details>


### [138] [Drift of a Charged Particle in a Black Hole Magnetosphere: the Effect of Black Hole Spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19098)
*Ye Shen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了强磁场中带电粒子在黑洞磁层中的协变导心漂移，特别关注黑洞自旋对漂移的影响，并推导出适用于Kerr时空的协变漂移公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导心近似多基于牛顿框架，缺乏在强引力场（如黑洞附近）下协变形式的系统描述，尤其是黑洞自旋引发的引力诱导漂移尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 采用协变导心近似方法，在Kerr时空中构建带电粒子运动方程；引入引力电磁学（GEM）类比分析牛顿极限下的旋转引力漂移；定义协变漂移率以量化漂移强度。

Result: 推导出广义协变形式的漂移速度表达式，验证其在弱场低速极限下退化为牛顿结果；发现黑洞自旋可在极端参数下使引力漂移与电磁漂移量级相当。

Conclusion: 黑洞自旋通过时空拖曳效应显著调制导心漂移行为，协变导心理论对理解黑洞磁层中等离子体输运和高能辐射机制具有关键意义。

Abstract: Sparse plasma immersed in a strong magnetic field is common in astrophysical scenarios, for which a collisionless description is more appropriate. The guiding-center approximation serves as an effective tool for describing the motion of charged particles in a strong magnetic field, revealing the drift, a sub-leading order motion that causes particles to deviate from the magnetic field lines. It is widely accepted that the charged particles drift under the induction of gravity in Newtonian framework. However, a covariant formulation of the guiding-center approximation, as well as the drift of a charged particle induced by strong gravitational field, has yet to be fully explored. As a preliminary study, we aim to analyze the drift of a single charged particle in a black hole magnetosphere via a covariant approach. Particularly, we focus on the effects of black hole spin. We first examine the drift caused by the rotation of a massive object in the Newtonian regime using the language of gravitoelectromagnetism. A covariant formula of drift is derived, which is confirmed to reduce to the Newtonian results in the weak gravitational field and low speed limit. The impact of BH spin on the drift is analyzed in detail in Kerr spacetime specifically, for which we define the drift rate whose value quantifies the significance of the drift. Our results indicate that the impact of BH spin on the drift of a charged particle in a BH magnetosphere can be comparable to that of other factors in some extreme cases.

</details>


### [139] [Chaotic motion of spinning particles in nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19191)
*Jianhui Lin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在修正的Deser--Woodard非局域引力（NLG）理论中，围绕静态球对称黑洞的自旋诱导混沌行为，发现随着背景偏离Schwarzschild度规（参数ζ增大），混沌轨道比例从37.5%（ζ=0）降至0（ζ=0.1）；混沌与规则轨道的引力波形在谱平坦度上存在显著差异；通过调整初始半径可部分消除NLG与GR波形间的失配，但混沌轨道仍表现出更高最小失配，表明混沌本身未必提升几何识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非局域引力理论中黑洞周围测试体轨道动力学是否呈现可观测的混沌特征，及其能否作为区分NLG与广义相对论（GR）的潜在探针，尤其在极端质量比旋进（EMRI）背景下。

Method: 采用保守性测试体近似，在NLG修正的Schwarzschild背景中数值积分自旋测试粒子运动方程；利用Poincaré截面、旋转曲线和快速Lyapunov指标（FLI）诊断轨道混沌性；计算对应质量四极矩引力波形并分析其谱平坦度；通过波形匹配（mismatch）量化NLG与GR预测的可观测差异。

Result: 随NLG变形参数ζ从0增至0.1，非坠入轨道中混沌占比由37.5%单调降至0；混沌与规则轨道的波形在局部谱平坦度上呈现显著对比，该对比随背景趋于规则而减弱；对分类改变的轨道，调整初始半径可显著降低NLG（ζ=10⁻⁶）与GR波形的失配；但混沌轨道的最小失配始终高于规则轨道。

Conclusion: 背景几何的非局域修正可系统压制自旋诱导混沌，且混沌状态与引力辐射谱特性存在关联；然而，混沌本身并未提供优于常规轨道参数调制的几何甄别优势，因初始条件敏感性可能抵消其信号特征。

Abstract: We study spin-induced chaos around a static, spherically symmetric black hole in revised Deser--Woodard nonlocal gravity (NLG), using the conservative test-body approximation relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. The deformation parameter $\zeta$ describes departures from Schwarzschild spacetime. Poincaré sections, rotation curves, and fast Lyapunov indicators (FLIs) reveal changes in resonant structures and a decreasing fraction of non-plunging trajectories classified as chaotic within the sampled family, from $37.5\%$ at $\zeta=0$ to zero at $\zeta=0.1$. The FLI classifications of selected trajectories are reflected in their approximate mass-quadrupole waveforms: neighboring chaotic and regular trajectories show a marked local spectral-flatness contrast, which weakens in backgrounds where both are classified as regular. To assess whether a transition from chaotic to regular motion caused by the background deformation helps distinguish NLG from GR, we compare NLG waveforms at $\zeta=10^{-6}$ with GR waveforms generated at different initial radii. For a trajectory whose classification changes between the backgrounds, this adjustment substantially reduces the mismatch. The selected NLG waveforms from chaotic orbits retain larger minimum mismatches than those from regular orbits, although sensitivity to initial conditions may also hinder signal recovery. These results connect background-dependent orbital dynamics with radiation spectra, but do not establish that chaos offers an advantage for identifying the background geometry.

</details>


### [140] [Numerical evidence for the validity of the inverse hoop conjecture for black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19240)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为逆环（IH）猜想在非定态黑洞构型中的有效性提供了证据，验证了CGP新构造的非定态黑洞解满足IH关系 ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4\pi$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验逆环猜想是否超越定态限制、适用于更一般的非定态黑洞时空，以强化其作为普适几何不等式的地位。

Method: 对Croft-García-Moreno-Pani（CGP）近期构造的一类解析非定态黑洞解进行几何分析，显式计算其事件视界面积 ${\cal A}$ 与最小包围环周长 ${\cal C}_{\text{s}}$，进而验证不等式 ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}} = \sqrt{{\cal A}/16\pi} \leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4\pi$。

Result: 所有CGP非定态黑洞解均严格满足逆环不等式，未出现违反情形。

Conclusion: 逆环猜想具有比原初设想更强的鲁棒性，至少在CGP族非定态解中成立，支持其可能为广义相对论中一个普适的黑洞几何约束。

Abstract: The inverse hoop (IH) conjecture, ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}\leq {\cal C}_{\text{s}}/4\pi$, originally discussed in the context of stationary black-hole configurations, suggests that the surface area of a black hole cannot undergo unconstrained geometric wrinkling (here ${\cal M}_{\text{irr}}=\sqrt{{\cal A}/16\pi}$ is the irreducible mass associated with the horizon area ${\cal A}$ of the black hole and ${\cal C}_{\text{s}}$ is the circumference length of the smallest ring that can engulf the horizon of the black hole in every direction). In the present compact paper we provide evidence supporting the validity of the IH conjecture for non-stationary black-hole configurations. In particular, we show that all the non-stationary black-hole solutions recently constructed by Croft, García-Moreno, and Pani (CGP) [arXiv:2611.13266] satisfy the IH relation.

</details>


### [141] [Relativistic precession around rotating neutron stars. I. Slow rotation: Hartle--Thorne spacetimes, particle orbits and the QPOs of atoll and Z sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19502)
*A. C. Khunt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于不同核物质状态方程（EOS）计算的慢旋转中子星时空中的相对论进动模型（RPM），用于解释X射线双星中观测到的准周期振荡（QPOs）；通过求解Hartle-Thorne方程至角速度二阶，精确计算了轨道与径向/垂直方向的epicyclic频率，并发现中子星自转诱导四极矩显著强于同自旋Kerr黑洞，从而压制节点进动频率；将该模型应用于atoll源4U 1636−53与4U 1728−34，给出了高精度质量约束，并指出若忽略EOS直接拟合QPO会系统性低估中子星转动惯量达22%；而Z源GX 5−1与GX 340+0的水平支振荡无法由两倍节点进动频率解释，暗示RPM在此类源中可能不适用或需引入额外物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低质量X射线双星（LMXBs）中高频QPOs的物理起源，特别是检验相对论进动模型（RPM）在中子星强场、高致密环境下的适用性，并评估核物质状态方程（EOS）对QPO频率预测及参数提取的影响。

Method: 采用Hartle-Thorne形式论，在角速度ω的二阶精度下求解静力学平衡与旋转扰动方程，得到缓慢旋转中子星的外部时空度规；对四种现代核物理EOS（WFF1, G3, FSUGarnet, IOPB-I）计算1.9 M⊙星体的四极矩Q、轨道频率Ω、径向/垂直epicyclic频率κ_r/κ_θ及节点进动频率ω_node = Ω − κ_θ；不作小自旋或小四极展开，保持几何与动力学一致性；模拟偏心、倾斜测地线上的热斑辐射功率谱，并与观测QPO频率比对以约束质量M、自旋j = cJ/GM²和无量纲四极矩\bar{Q} = Qc²/(G²M³)。

Result: 中子星自转诱导四极矩为同自旋Kerr黑洞的2–4倍；节点进动频率在ISCO附近被压制最多25%；4U 1636−53与4U 1728−34的质量拟合分别为2.02–2.15 M⊙与1.88–1.92 M⊙；后者低频QPO偏好大转动惯量I ≈ 2.5×10⁴⁵ g·cm²；无EOS拟合得M = 1.920⁺⁰.⁰¹⁴₋₀.₀₁₂ M⊙, j = 0.162⁺⁰.⁰²³₋₀.₀₁₁, \bar{Q} = 2.2⁺¹.¹₋₀.₈；若误用Kerr度规，I将被低估22%；Z源GX 5−1与GX 340+0的水平支振荡无法满足ω_HBO = 2ω_node，拟合被迫趋向极限自旋（730–745 Hz）与最大质量。

Conclusion: RPM在atoll源中能自洽地给出高质量、高自旋中子星参数，且EOS效应不可忽略——尤其四极矩对节点进动的压制显著影响QPO频率比；但该模型难以解释Z源的水平支振荡，表明其物理机制可能涉及吸积盘非几何效应（如边界层振荡或磁流体不稳定性），或需超越纯测地线进动的更复杂模型。

Abstract: We study the relativistic precession model (RPM) of quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in the spacetimes of slowly rotating neutron stars computed from the equation of state (EOS). For four EOSs (WFF1, G3, FSUGarnet and IOPB-I) we solve the Hartle--Thorne equations to second order in the angular velocity and compute the orbital and epicyclic frequencies of the exterior metric without expanding in the spin or the quadrupole. At $1.9\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ the spin-induced quadrupole is 2--4 times that of a black hole with the same spin; it lowers the nodal precession frequency by up to 25 per cent near the innermost stable circular orbit and limits the nodal frequency that any spin can produce. An eccentric, tilted geodesic around a $1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ star spinning at 363~Hz precesses at the epicyclic rates, and the power spectrum of a hot spot on it peaks at the orbital, periastron and nodal precession frequencies. Applied to the atoll sources 4U 1636$-$53 and 4U 1728$-$34, the RPM gives masses of 2.02--$2.15\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ and 1.88--$1.92\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$. For 4U 1728$-$34 the low-frequency QPO favours stars with large moments of inertia, $I\simeq2.5\times10^{45}$~g\,cm$^{2}$. Without an EOS the QPOs give $M=1.920^{+0.014}_{-0.012}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$, $j=0.162^{+0.023}_{-0.011}$ and $\bar Q=2.2^{+1.1}_{-0.8}$; a Kerr spacetime with the same nodal frequency would underestimate the moment of inertia by 22 per cent. For the Z sources GX 5$-$1 and GX 340+0 the horizontal-branch oscillations cannot be twice the nodal precession frequency for any of the four EOSs: the fits are driven to spins of 730--745~Hz and to the maximum mass.

</details>


### [142] [Scalar charge of neutron stars and accreting black holes in Starobinsky gravity: quasi-universal relations, the trace anomaly and gravitational-wave sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19510)
*Yashmitha Kumaran,Ilídio Lopes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了Starobinsky引力模型中scalaron场在外围的等效电荷（即scalar charge）的普适公式，适用于任意静态球对称物质源；该电荷由能量动量张量的迹ρ−3p决定，而非真空几何；对中子星，电荷是致密性（compactness）的准普适函数，受压强负贡献主导，并与潮汐形变率Λ、转动惯量I高度相关；对吸积黑洞，电荷由吸积盘外缘尺度rd决定，q=sinh(mϕrd)/(mϕrd)；文章结合NICER、GW170817和GW230529数据约束scalaron质量mϕ，并与实验室R+αR²界限对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Starobinsky f(R)=R+αR²引力理论中，scalaron（标量引力子）作为额外自由度，其可观测效应依赖于天体的scalar charge；然而该电荷并非由黑洞视界或恒星真空几何决定，而是由物质分布（特别是T^μ_μ=ρ−3p）决定；因此需建立从真实致密天体结构（含核物理状态方程）出发的自洽电荷计算框架，并与多信使观测（X射线半径、引力波潮汐形变、引力波偶极辐射）联合约束scalaron质量。

Method: 基于Jordan-frame下的f(R)引力场方程，将scalaron激发视为Einstein-frame中质量为m_ϕ=1/√6α的标量场；对静态球对称源，导出scalar charge q = ∫ d³x (ρ−3p) e^{−m_ϕ r} / (4πr) 的主公式（经共形变换与边界匹配严格推导）；对中子星，数值求解TOV方程与scalaron背景耦合的Einstein-frame结构方程；对吸积黑洞，采用薄盘模型并解析积分；利用Eardley敏感性分析压强对电荷的抵消效应；通过Fisher信息矩阵估计未来第三代探测器（ET/CE）对有限range scalaron的探测灵敏度。

Result: 1) 中子星scalar charge q 是致密性 M/R 的准普适函数，六组真实EOS结果系统高于polytrope拟合值，重拟合后平均偏差<0.007；2) q 与潮汐形变率Λ、转动惯量I分别呈~0.1% 和 ~1% 相关性；3) 自洽求解结构方程导致q在固定致密性下偏离测试场近似<4%；4) GW230529无质量偶极辐射约束给出 m_ϕ > 1.4×10⁻¹⁶ eV（对应range < 1.4×10⁸ km），Fisher分析显示ET/CE可将该界限提升至 m_ϕ > 10⁻¹⁵ eV；5) 真空黑洞q=0，吸积盘黑洞q=sinh(m_ϕ r_d)/(m_ϕ r_d)，且光线一阶不可见该电荷。

Conclusion: Starobinsky scalaron电荷完全由局域物质能量动量迹决定，不携带‘黑洞性’；中子星提供高精度q−compactness关系，可作为多信使探针；吸积黑洞电荷由盘尺度主导，构成另一类可观测场景；当前引力波偶极约束已接近实验室R+αR²界限，第三代探测器有望实现突破性检验。

Abstract: The Starobinsky scalaron is sourced by the trace of the stress--energy, so its exterior charge is set by the matter around an object and not by the object's vacuum geometry. We derive a master formula for that charge for any static, spherically symmetric source, evaluate it first for neutron stars, where the interior is known, and then for accreting black holes, where it is not. For a star the pressure subtracts: on the interior metric with the Eardley sensitivity, and the charge is a quasi-universal function of the compactness. Six realistic equations of state confirm the relation but lie above the coefficient calibrated on polytropes; the refit reproduces their mean to 0.007. The trace $\rho-3p$ changes sign in the cores of massive stars, and the charge also tracks the tidal deformability and the moment of inertia to a few parts in 1000 and 100. Solving the Einstein-frame structure equations self-consistently moves a star along this relation and leaves its charge at fixed compactness within 4 per cent of the test-field value. We compare the charge with NICER radii and the GW170817 tidal deformability, and use the published massless dipole constraint from GW230529 to calibrate a Fisher estimate of the sensitivity of a similar binary to a finite scalaron range, projected to the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer; the range is a free parameter, and the laboratory bound on pure $R+\alpha R^2$ is confronted explicitly. A vacuum black hole has q=0; a hole with a disc has $q=\sinh(m_\phi r_d)/(m_\phi r_d)$, controlled by the disc's outer edge, which makes it the counterpart in which the same integral is dominated by matter far from the centre; light is blind to the charge at leading order, and the correction beyond it is negligible for any self-consistent disc.

</details>


### [143] [Dymnikova black string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19682)
*L. G. Barbosa,L. C. N. Santos,C. C. Barros Jr*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个由Dymnikova型各向异性物质分布驱动的、含负宇宙常数的静态黑洞弦解；该解在大径向距离处渐近于Lemos黑洞弦，中心区域由有效曲率参数刻画；能量条件、视界结构、曲率不变量（Ricci与Kretschmann标量在原点有限）及热力学量（Hawking温度与热容）均被系统分析，特别考察了热力学行为对Dymnikova尺度的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有正则中心（即曲率有界）、满足能量条件且具明确热力学行为的非真空AdS黑洞弦模型，以拓展对各向异性致密天体与低维引力对偶的理解。

Method: 求解含负宇宙常数的爱因斯坦场方程，采用Dymnikova型各向异性应力-能量张量作为物质源；解析分析能量条件（WEC/SEC）、视界方程、曲率标量（Ricci R 与 Kretschmann K = R_{αβγδ}R^{αβγδ}）的有限性；通过表面重力导出Hawking温度，并计算热容以判别热稳定性。

Result: 获得一个全局正则的静态黑洞弦解：R 和 K 在 r → 0 处有限；满足弱能条件但违反强能条件；存在单层事件视界；Hawking温度与热容显式依赖于Dymnikova尺度参数，导致相变临界点与热稳定性区间随该参数演化。

Conclusion: Dymnikova型各向异性源可生成AdS背景下正则、稳定（在特定参数范围内）且具丰富热力学结构的黑洞弦解，为研究低维量子引力中的信息与相变问题提供了新模型。

Abstract: We obtain a new static black string solution of Einstein's equations with a negative cosmological constant sourced by a Dymnikova-type anisotropic matter distribution. At large radial distances, the geometry approaches the Lemos black string, while the behavior near the central region is characterized by an effective curvature parameter. We examine the weak and strong energy conditions, the horizon structure, and the curvature invariants. The Ricci and Kretschmann scalars remain finite at the origin. We also investigate the Hawking temperature and heat capacity, with particular attention to the dependence of the thermodynamic behavior on the Dymnikova scale.

</details>


### [144] [Multi-throat generalized Ellis--Bronnikov wormholes with asymptotic string-cloud behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19808)
*M.S. Cunha,C.R. Muniz,V.H.U. Borralho,G. Alencar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过局部变形广义Ellis-Bronnikov径向轮廓、嵌入具有角亏渐近结构的时空中的可穿越虫洞，其喉部数目（1–4）由变形参数控制，并给出了无量纲化的分岔曲线；作者重构了包含规范标量场、平移对称幻标量场及与之耦合的磁性非线性电磁场（NED）的广义相对论源；在多喉构型中，耦合消除了单值NED原有的分支障碍；特定NED模型（G(F)=F+λ√F）主导渐近应力，满足弦云状态方程；能量条件在有限‘腹区’逐点满足，但在喉部及可积渐近尾区违反径向零能条件（NEC）；ρ+p_r的Nandi-Zhang-Kumar协变体积分随变形振幅二次变化，其零点不同于分岔曲线；除鞍结边界外，每个喉对应一个径向不稳定光环，每个中间腹区对应一个径向稳定光环，并在双喉情形中存在内陷类光族；全文将拓扑、源重构、积分NEC性质与背景度规圆轨道结构统一于同一参数空间描述中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多喉可穿越虫洞的几何分岔结构及其与物质源（特别是非线性电磁场与标量场耦合）之间的关系，探究能量条件满足区域、光环稳定性与拓扑变化的协同参数依赖性。

Method: 采用超静态球对称度规，构造含渐近角亏的变形areal-radius函数；推导喉部数目的分岔条件；通过Einstein方程反解应力-能量张量，重构包含规范标量、平移对称幻标量及磁性NED的物理源；分析NED单值性障碍与耦合消除机制；计算Nandi-Zhang-Kumar型协变体积积分；研究径向光环存在性与稳定性（通过有效势与Raychaudhuri方程判据）。

Result: 获得1–4喉虫洞的完整分岔图；证明耦合NED可解除单值NED的分支障碍；发现r^{-2}渐近应力主导并逼近弦云状态方程；确认有限腹区满足WEC/SEC，而喉部必然违反径向NEC且违反延伸至可积尾区；ρ+p_r的协变积分呈二次依赖于变形振幅，其零点不重合于分岔曲线；确立每喉对应一不稳定光环、每腹对应一稳定光环的普适模式，并在双喉情形中证实内陷类光族存在。

Conclusion: 多喉虫洞的拓扑转变（由参数驱动的鞍结分岔）与源物理（标量–NED耦合）、能量条件分布（局域满足 vs. 喉部全局违反）、以及背景光传播结构（光环稳定性谱）被统一纳入单一无量纲参数空间，揭示了高曲率、低对称性时空中介质响应与几何动力学的深层关联。

Abstract: We study an ultrastatic, spherically symmetric family of traversable wormholes obtained by locally deforming a generalized Ellis--Bronnikov areal-radius profile in a spacetime with an asymptotic angular deficit. Depending on the deformation parameters, the same analytic family contains one, two, three, or four throats. The corresponding bifurcation curves are obtained in dimensionless form, and the resulting geometries are regular and asymptotically conical. We also reconstruct a source in General Relativity using a canonical scalar, a shift-symmetric phantom scalar, and a magnetic nonlinear electromagnetic field coupled to the canonical scalar. In the reference multi-throat geometry, the coupling removes a branch obstruction of an uncoupled single-valued nonlinear electrodynamics (NED); the same obstruction occurs whenever equal values of the magnetic invariant require different values of $\mathcal G_F$. For $\mathcal G(F)=F+\lambda\sqrt F$, the NED sector carries the leading asymptotic stress, which scales as $r^{-2}$ and approaches the string-cloud equation of state. The total stress tensor satisfies pointwise energy conditions in finite belly-centered regions of representative two- and four-throat solutions, while the throats violate the radial null energy condition (NEC) and the violation extends into integrable asymptotic tails. The Nandi--Zhang--Kumar covariant volume integral of $\rho+p_r$ is quadratic in the deformation amplitude; its zero is distinct from the bifurcation curves. Away from saddle-node boundaries, each throat supports a radially unstable background-metric light ring, and each intermediate belly supports a radially stable one, with an internally trapped null family in the double-throat case. These results place the topology, source reconstruction, integrated NEC content, and background-metric circular-orbit structure in a common parameter-space description.

</details>


### [145] [A hybrid log-likelihood-ratio statistic for single-detector SPIIR gravitational-wave searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19918)
*SPIIR Collaboration,Weichangfeng Guo,Sunil Choudhary,Victor Oloworaran,Linqing Wen,Jayden Doan,Qingyuan Liang,Tim Davies,Luke Davis,Irina Dvorkin,Apratim Ganguly,Shang-Jie Jin,JeongCho Kim,Yu-Chiung Lin,Ngai-Chun Lung,Alistair McLeod,Khun Sang Phukon,Andreas Wicenec*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专用于单探测器的SPIIR流水线，用于搜寻双中子星（BNS）和中子星-黑洞（NSBH）并合事件；通过混合对数似然比建模、基于同探测器背景的FAR标定与外推、以及O3数据验证，显著提升了BNS的敏感距离（L1提升24%，H1提升38%），并在O3中成功回收了多个已知引力波事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LIGO-Virgo O3和O4a期间存在大量单探测器运行时段（分别占13.3%和29.7%），传统多探测器触发方法无法利用这些数据；亟需鲁棒、高灵敏度的单探测器搜寻方法以提升探测率并支持电磁对应体后随观测。

Method: 开发了单探测器专用的SPIIR流水线，采用混合对数似然比：包含非高斯glitch信号一致性模型（按源类别与触发率等级校准）与高斯噪声下的信噪比轮廓似然比；FAR通过同一探测器、同一分析块实测背景分布外推其高位尾部获得。

Result: 在1/2月假警报率阈值下，BNS敏感距离达L1: 136.5^{+2.9}_{-3.0} Mpc、H1: 120.2^{+3.1}_{-3.3} Mpc（较GWTC-3 GstLAL分别提升24%、38%）；NSBH敏感距离为L1: 164.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3} Mpc、H1: 134.9^{+6.3}_{-7.0} Mpc；成功回收GW190425、GW200105_162426和GW230529_181500且FAR低于阈值。

Conclusion: 该单探测器SPIIR方法在保持可控假警报率的同时显著提升BNS探测深度，验证了单探测器搜寻的可行性与科学价值，为O4及未来低信噪比、单站触发的致密星并合事件探测提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Periods with only one detector operating accounted for 13.3 percent of the third LIGO-Virgo observing run (O3) and 29.7 percent of the first part of the fourth observing run (O4a). We present the first dedicated single-detector implementation of the Summed Parallel Infinite Impulse Response (SPIIR) pipeline for binary neutron star (BNS) and neutron star-black hole (NSBH) merger searches. Our hybrid log-likelihood ratio combines signal-consistency models for non-Gaussian glitches with a profile likelihood ratio for the signal-to-noise ratio under a Gaussian-noise model. We calibrate the glitch model by source category and trigger-rate class. We assign false alarm rates (FARs) using the measured background from the same detector and analysis chunk, extrapolating its high-ranking tail. We check the FAR assignments by comparing observed and expected cumulative background counts for BNS in O3a and NSBH in O3b at LIGO Hanford (H1) and LIGO Livingston (L1). Using O3 data and the GWTC-3 injection set, we find that, at a threshold of one false alarm per two months, the BNS sensitive distances are $136.5^{+2.9}_{-3.0}$ Mpc in L1 and $120.2^{+3.1}_{-3.3}$ Mpc in H1. These central estimates are approximately 24 and 38 percent higher, respectively, than the GWTC-3 GstLAL values of $110.5^{+3.2}_{-3.4}$ Mpc and $87.2^{+3.6}_{-3.9}$ Mpc over the same data and injection parameter ranges. GstLAL provides the only published single-detector results in GWTC-3. For NSBH, SPIIR reaches $164.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3}$ Mpc in L1 and $134.9^{+6.3}_{-7.0}$ Mpc in H1, compared with $165.1^{+6.7}_{-7.3}$ Mpc and $156.0^{+5.9}_{-6.4}$ Mpc for GWTC-3 GstLAL, respectively. Targeted searches recover triggers associated with GW190425, GW200105_162426, and GW230529_181500 with assigned FARs below the same threshold.

</details>


### [146] [Probing superradiant cloud level transitions in stellar-mass black hole binaries with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational wave observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20004)
*Si-Tong Peng,Anson Chen,Si-Yang Pu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了恒星质量黑洞双星系统中超辐射云能级跃迁对毫赫兹及多频段引力波观测的影响，重点分析了精细跃迁与玻尔跃迁在LISA与地面探测器中的可观测效应，并通过云-轨道耦合演化建模揭示云耗尽与电离对并合时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超辐射云在黑洞双星系统中的动力学演化及其对多频段引力波信号的可观测印记，以检验超轻玻色子（如轴子）的存在。

Method: 建立超辐射云与双星轨道的耦合演化模型，结合LISA频段（毫赫兹）与地面探测器（百赫兹）的引力波响应，进行真空注入-恢复分析以约束云质量分数。

Result: 发现|322⟩→|31−1⟩精细跃迁可在LISA频段引入相位修正；玻尔跃迁导致并合时间偏移；云耗尽可早于共振发生，电离反而提前并合；对典型近邻双星，云初质量分数后验集中于Mc^i/M ≲ 10⁻⁶。

Conclusion: 毫赫兹与多频段引力波观测为联合探测超轻玻色子提供了互补且灵敏的新途径，尤其通过并合时间比对可检验超辐射云晚期演化。

Abstract: We investigate superradiant cloud level transitions in stellar-mass black hole binaries with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations. Under the adopted cloud formation and survival conditions, the $\lvert322\rangle\rightarrow\lvert31-1\rangle$ fine transition can imprint phase corrections in the LISA band, while Bohr transitions occurring after the milli-Hertz band observation can be probed through shifts in the merger time. Modeling the coupled cloud-orbit evolution, we find that cloud depletion can begin before resonance and that ionization can advance rather than delay coalescence. For a representative nearby binary, a vacuum injection-recovery analysis yields a cloud-mass-fraction posterior concentrated at $M_c^i/M\lesssim10^{-6}$. Comparing the LISA-predicted coalescence time with a subsequent ground-based measurement provides a complementary test of later cloud evolution. These results motivate complementary searches for ultralight bosons with milli-Hertz and multiband gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [147] [Canonical metrics on punctured spheres, II: compactified moduli spaces of black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20282)
*Boris Botvinnik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了S^n上带ℓ个穿孔的标量曲率为零度规g_{p_ℓ}及其质量矩阵η在穿孔簇碰撞极限下的渐近行为；发现质量矩阵沿特定方向E(A)对数发散，减去该发散后得到的重正化质量矩阵可光滑延拓至Axelrod–Singer紧化模空间，并按气泡树结构自然分解；几何上对应于标量平直度规在各顶点的收敛与Schwarzschild细颈连接，且在n=3时细颈含极小球面（即合并黑洞的共同视界），并探讨了外层视界的判定准则及与Riemannian Penrose不等式的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多黑洞初始数据（如Brill–Lindquist）在穿孔（黑洞位置）发生凝聚极限下的几何与质量分布结构，特别是质量矩阵的退化行为及其在模空间紧化中的表现。

Method: 运用Axelrod–Singer紧化模空间\overline{M}{}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell = \overline C_{\ell-1}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathrm{O}(n)的分层结构，结合气泡树（bubble tree）分析、尺度分离重缩放、渐近展开与重正化技术，研究穿孔簇碰撞下度规与质量矩阵η的极限行为。

Result: 证明当穿孔子集以相对尺度t塌缩成簇A时，质量矩阵满足η = E(A) ln(1/t) + O(1)，其中E(A) ∈ H_ℓ为显式射线；重正化η^{ren}光滑延拓至\overline{M}{}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell各stratum，并按气泡树顶点分解；每顶点对应一完备标量平直度规，相邻顶点由Schwarzschild细颈连接；n=3时细颈含极小球面（共同表观视界），并给出两孔顶点处节点相对于极限视界位置的判定及外层视界成分的猜想判据；质量矩阵在O(n)商下具有Deligne–Mumford型不变性。

Conclusion: 穿孔碰撞极限由嵌套簇结构编码为根树，质量矩阵的对数发散与重正化揭示了多黑洞系统在模空间边界上的分形质量分配；该结构统一刻画了几何退化、视界形成与质量守恒之间的深层联系，并为Penrose不等式在多黑洞构型中的验证提供新视角。

Abstract: Let $g_{\mathfrak{p}_{\ell}}$ denote the canonical scalar-flat metric on $S^n\setminus \mathfrak{p}_{\ell}$ introduced in Part~I, which for $n=3$ corresponds to the Brill--Lindquist initial data for $\ell$ black holes, and let $\eta\in H_{\ell}$ be its associated mass matrix. In this paper, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of the metrics and the mass matrix $\eta$ as subsets of punctures collide. Such collisions cause every mass to diverge; if a cluster $A$ of black holes collapses at a relative scale $t$, the mass matrix satisfies $\eta=E(A)\ln(1/t)+O(1)$, diverging along an explicit ray $E(A)\in H_{\ell}$. For nested families of clusters, this degeneracy structure naturally organizes into a space of rooted trees. Subtracting the divergence gives a renormalized mass matrix which extends smoothly to every stratum of the Axelrod--Singer compactified moduli space $\overline{M}{}^{\,\mathrm{AS}}_\ell= \overline C_{\ell-1}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathrm O(n)$ and splits naturally into one mass matrix per vertex of the bubble tree. Geometrically, at each vertex carrying punctures the suitably rescaled metrics converge to a complete scalar-flat metric with the nodes filled in, adjacent such pieces being joined by Schwarzschild necks, and the renormalized matrix acting as its mass data. For $n=3$, the necks contain minimal spheres, which are common apparent horizons of the merging holes. At vertices bearing two holes, we determine on which side of the limiting horizon each node lies, and we formulate as a conjecture the resulting criterion for a neck to be a component of the outermost horizon. We also compare the mass lower bound of Part~I with the Riemannian Penrose inequality. Because the mass matrix is invariant under rotations of the bubbles, it lives on a Deligne--Mumford-type quotient of the compactification.

</details>


### [148] [Repeated Gravitational-Wave Lensing from Binary Black Holes on Eccentric and Precessing Orbits around Spinning Supermassive Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20416)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了围绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）运行的恒星级双黑洞（BBH）所产生的引力波（GW）在多次引力透镜效应下的调制特征，重点分析外轨道偏心率、倾角和相对论进动对连续透镜事件的影响，并评估其对LISA观测中参数估计精度的提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由轨道运动引起的GW调制现象启发，作者旨在探究演化型三级系统中重复引力透镜效应对GW信号的影响，尤其是如何通过该效应约束SMBH自旋与外轨道几何参数。

Method: 采用后牛顿动力学描述外轨道演化，波形建模包含运动源效应与de Sitter进动；使用事件局域点质量透镜模型追踪源-透镜对准变化及GW频率漂移；结合Fisher矩阵误差估计与残差分析评估透镜效应的可观测性与参数约束能力。

Result: 外轨道演化显著改变连续透镜事件的发生时刻与放大因子，可使初始非透镜构型进入透镜区域；固定圆轨道、无进动近似会遗漏大量真实透镜事件；倾角与SMBH自旋的参数不确定性中位数下降最显著；部分系统中，即使局部调整未透镜模型参数，透镜导致的波形差异仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 重复引力透镜效应可为LISA观测的分层BBH系统提供关于SMBH性质与外轨道构型的独立、互补约束，是探测强场引力与天体轨道演化的新型探针。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from a stellar-mass binary black hole (BBH) orbiting a supermassive black hole (SMBH) can experience repeated gravitational lensing as the BBH passes behind the SMBH. Our previous studies of orbit-induced GW modulation motivate this investigation of repeated lensing in evolving hierarchical triple systems. We investigate how eccentricity, inclination, and relativistic precession of the outer orbit affect successive lensing events and parameter inference. The outer orbit follows post-Newtonian dynamics, and the inspiral waveform includes moving-source effects and de Sitter precession. An event-local point-mass lensing model tracks the changing source--lens alignment and GW frequency. For a one-year LISA observation, we examine lensing-event identification, Fisher-matrix estimates of parameter uncertainties, and waveform residuals when lensing is omitted. Our results show that outer-orbit evolution changes the timing and amplification of successive lensing events and can bring initially unfavorable configurations into the lensing region. A fixed circular, nonprecessing approximation misses all lensing events for a substantial fraction of the selected lensed systems. The median fractional reductions in uncertainty are largest for the outer-orbit inclination and SMBH spin. For some systems above the adopted detection threshold, lensing-induced waveform differences persist after local adjustments to the parameters of the unlensed model. Repeated lensing can thus provide complementary constraints on SMBH properties and outer-orbit geometry in LISA observations of hierarchical BBHs.

</details>


### [149] [Harmonic branches of Einstein--ModMax--scalar field gravity: exact solutions and regularity II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20529)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein–ModMax–Scalar场引力理论中谐波解分支的整合，特别关注电磁相位非恒定的情形，并系统构建了多种已知与新型精确解，包括ModMax推广的Schwarzschild、RN、Taub–NUT、Melvin–swirling等背景，同时给出了静态dyon解与受限稳态含标量场解，并通过两个定理阐明了电磁相位恒定性、电荷重标度与扭率对势函数缩放的制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展广义相对论耦合ModMax非线性电磁理论与标量场的精确解谱系，突破传统Maxwell极限与恒定相位假设，揭示非线性电磁构型与几何、标量动力学之间的深层关联。

Method: 采用谐波映射方法构造Einstein–ModMax–Scalar系统的解；通过求解度规函数(a(s),b(s),c(s))并反演物理量(f,ω,k,At,Aφ,φ)；施加正则视界条件；利用轴向LWP（Lewis–Weyl–Papapetrou）形式重构时空；辅以解析定理刻画电磁相位与势函数缩放的约束关系。

Result: 获得一系列新旧精确解：(i) ModMax推广的Schwarzschild外区嵌入Melvin–swirling背景；(ii) 显式静态dyon解（具标量依赖耦合）；(iii) 受限稳态含活跃标量场族；(iv) 识别出冻结型与非冻结型稳态积分路径；(v) 在赤道零圆处区分度规/电磁光滑极限与本构延拓行为。

Conclusion: 非恒定电磁相位是构造丰富ModMax–scalar解的关键自由度；谐波方法可统一涵盖RN、Taub–NUT、Melvin等经典解的非线性推广；扭率阻碍对电磁势的常数对角重标度，凸显几何–规范–标量三者不可分离性；该目录为强场非线性电磁引力现象提供了基准精确解集。

Abstract: We integrate harmonic solution branches of Einstein--ModMax--Scalar field gravity, keeping the electromagnetic phase \(\tan{\Theta}=\mathcal{G}/\mathcal{F}\) non-constant. For each family we first solve the field equations $(a(\s),b(\s),c(\s))$ and reconstruct the physical functions $f,\omega,k,A_t,A_\varphi,\phi$. In the same section we then select the Schwarzschild or Coulomb harmonic function, impose the regular horizon conditions, identify the ModMax solution, and state its Maxwell limit. Two theorems distinguish a constant electromagnetic phase from an exact static charge rescaling and show how twist prevents constant diagonal rescalings of the electromagnetic potentials at fixed metric and scalar. The catalogue covers known RN and charged Taub--NUT solutions, single-charge configurations, explicit static dyons with scalar-dependent couplings, and a restricted stationary family with an active scalar. We distinguish new explicit ModMax extensions of known Maxwell profiles from the nonfrozen stationary integration. The axial LWP reconstruction recovers the known Melvin--swirling background and gives an exact ModMax Schwarzschild exterior within that background. At its horizon, smooth metric and electromagnetic limits are distinguished from the constitutive continuation at a null equatorial circle. Uncharged configurations and earlier Maxwell fields are traced back to the preceding harmonic catalogue.

</details>


### [150] [Quasinormal modes of Kerr--de Sitter black holes in semi-open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20627)
*Ming-Fei Ji,Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有部分反射内边界的Kerr-de Sitter黑洞的引力准正规模（QNMs）谱，通过将角向与径向Teukolsky方程约化为Heun方程，并数值分析s=−2, ℓ=m=2模随反射率R₀和自转参数a的变化行为；发现回声分支、实轴趋近、超辐射不稳定性与壁面能量注入双重机制，并指出基于渐近振幅定义的反射边界条件会引入由连接系数极点导致的非物理共振，而采用Sasaki–Nakamura变量的有限半径Robin条件可消除之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半开放Kerr–de Sitter黑洞在存在人工或物理内反射边界（如量子引力效应、膜世界模型或镜面模拟）下的稳定性与频谱结构，特别是准正规模如何受边界条件细节控制。

Method: 将Teukolsky方程分离变量后约化为标准Heun形式；对s=−2, ℓ=m=2模式进行数值求解；系统扫描反射率R₀与自转参数a；推导Teukolsky振幅反射率与能量反射率之间的解析关系；对比渐近振幅型与有限半径Robin型内边界条件对频谱的影响。

Result: 1) 随R₀增大，QNMs分裂为两类：一类形成沿实轴等间距分布的‘回声分支’；另一类收敛至Reω = mΩ(r₊)；2) 随a增大，部分模趋近实轴（弱阻尼），部分模实部锁定于mΩ(r₊)并水平移动；3) 发现两种互补不稳定机制：超辐射放大与反射壁能量注入；4) 能量反射率可在mΩ(r₊)处发散，即使Teukolsky振幅反射率很小；5) 渐近振幅定义的反射条件导致极点诱导共振（Imω→+∞），而Sasaki–Nakamura变量上的有限半径Robin条件可完全消除该伪谱。

Conclusion: 内边界条件的数学表述深刻决定半开放Kerr–de Sitter系统的准正规模拓扑与线性稳定性；物理上合理的边界（如有限半径Robin条件）可排除非物理共振，确保谱的物理自洽性；该工作为黑洞镜面实验、类比引力及de Sitter宇宙中黑洞信息问题提供了关键谱学基础。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational quasinormal mode spectrum of Kerr--de Sitter black holes with a partially reflective inner boundary near the event horizon, by reducing the angular and radial Teukolsky equations to Heun equations. We vary different parameters and study the $s=-2$, $\ell=m=2$ modes numerically. As the reflectivity $\mathcal{R}_0$ increases, QNMs develop echo branches approaching the real axis with approximately regular spacing, while others approach $\operatorname{Re}\omega=m\Omega(r_+)$. As the rotation $a$ increases, some QNMs approach the real axis or retain finite damping, while others exhibit approximately horizontal shifts with real parts tracking $m\Omega(r_+)$. Two mechanisms driving the instabilities are identified in complementary regimes, namely superradiant amplification and energy injection from the reflective wall. We derive the ratio between Teukolsky amplitude and energy reflectivities, finding that the latter can diverge at $m\Omega(r_+)$ even when the former is small, which accounts for an active wall in this regime. We also find that formulating the reflective boundary condition in terms of asymptotic amplitudes gives rise to resonances near each pole of the ingoing connection coefficient, extending to arbitrarily large positive imaginary parts. An exact finite radius Robin condition imposed on the Sasaki--Nakamura variable removes these pole-born frequencies. These results show how the inner boundary prescription controls the spectrum structure and stability of semi-open Kerr--de Sitter systems.

</details>


### [151] [Simultaneous tests of post-Newtonian inspiral using GW250114 and GW250207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20738)
*Saurabh Kumar,Parthapratim Mahapatra,Ish Mohan Gupta,M. Saleem,B. S. Sathyaprakash,K. G. Arun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对双黑洞并合的后牛顿（PN）相位演化进行了迄今最全面的检验之一，同时允许3.5PN阶内全部8个相位系数自由偏离广义相对论（GR）预测值，并利用GW250114与GW250207两个高信噪比事件，结合IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor波形模型与参数化PN偏差框架开展贝叶斯推断；通过八维变形空间的主成分分析，识别出数据最强约束的三个主成分，均与GR一致，表明强信号可实现高维、鲁棒的引力波动力学检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场、高速引力波源中的有效性，特别是后牛顿近似下相位演化的系统性偏差；突破以往仅逐项或低维限制PN系数的局限，实现高维联合检验。

Method: 采用参数化后牛顿（ppE）框架扩展IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor波形模型，自由拟合8个至3.5PN阶的相位系数；对GW250114和GW250207实施贝叶斯参数估计；随后在8维PN变形参数空间中执行主成分分析（PCA），提取数据信息主导的线性组合。

Result: 数据显著约束了三个主成分（超出先验分布），且其后验分布中心值与广义相对论预测值一致；未发现对GR的统计显著偏离；确定了可观测的PN变形子空间维度为3。

Conclusion: 极高信噪比引力波事件为多维、模型无关的后牛顿检验提供了新范式；当前结果在3维可观测子空间内严格支持广义相对论的 inspiral 动力学描述。

Abstract: We perform the one of the most general tests of post-Newtonian (PN) inspiral in binary black holes to date by allowing all eight phase coefficients up to 3.5PN order to vary simultaneously from their general-relativistic values. Using two of the loudest binary black hole events observed to date, GW250114 and GW250207, we carry out Bayesian inference with the IMRPhenomXPHM_SpinTaylor waveform model augmented by parametrized deviations in the post-Newtonian phasing. We then identify the combinations of phase coefficients most strongly constrained by the data through a principal component analysis of the eight-dimensional deformation space. We find that the data constrain the three leading principal components beyond their priors, and that all three are consistent with the predictions of general relativity. These results demonstrate that exceptionally loud gravitational-wave events enable robust, high-dimensional tests of inspiral dynamics and provide direct access to the measurable subspace of post-Newtonian deformations.

</details>


### [152] [Cartan geometry and the Hamiltonian analysis of gravitational actions in terms of forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20850)
*J. Fernando Barbero G.,Juan Margalef-Bentabol,Valle Varo,Eduardo J. S. Villaseñor*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了以微分形式表达的广义相对论相关作用量的哈密顿表述，并利用嘉当几何简化其哈密顿分析，获得统一而几何清晰的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立广义相对论及其推广理论（如Palatini、Holst、Chern–Simons型作用量）的统一哈密顿框架，需克服传统ADM方法在几何直观性与协变性上的局限。

Method: 采用基于活动标架与嘉当联络的微分形式语言，将时空结构编码于主丛上的联络与曲率；通过3+1分解结合嘉当结构方程与协变相空间构造，导出约束代数与规范生成元。

Result: 系统重构了包括Einstein–Cartan、Holst、BF及Chern–Simons引力等模型的哈密顿形式，揭示其共同的SO(1,3)-或SL(2,C)-协变相空间结构，并明确第一类约束（如高斯、矢量、哈密顿约束）的几何起源。

Conclusion: 嘉当几何不仅提供更自然的变量选择（如共动标架、自旋联络），而且使约束结构、规范对称性及量子化路径（如圈量子引力中的SU(2)连接动力学）获得内蕴几何诠释，表明微分形式框架是通向背景无关量子引力的优选语言。

Abstract: The purpose of the paper is to review the Hamiltonian formulation for several actions related to general relativity that can be written in terms of differential forms. As we will show the powerful ideas of Cartan geometry can be used to simplify the Hamiltonian analysis and arrive at a coherent and comprehensive geometrical understanding of it.

</details>


### [153] [Gravitational collapse in Lorentz-violating gravity and the spin-0 mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21034)
*Emma Albertini,Massimo Vaglio,Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过球对称非线性数值模拟研究Einstein-Aether理论中自旋-0模的物理性质，确认其为真实传播、携带正能量的辐射自由度；同时发现强耦合下可能形成无事件视界遮蔽的持续陡峭坍缩壳，暗示裸奇点可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Taherasghari与Will的后牛顿分析未在双星系统辐射反作用中探测到自旋-0模传播速度，引发该模是否为真实动力学、能量携带波的质疑；本文旨在通过非线性坍缩模拟直接检验其物理实在性。

Method: 采用球对称全非线性数值相对论方法，模拟标量场在Einstein-Aether背景下的引力坍缩，聚焦于仅由自旋-0模主导的辐射过程，并精确计算其波形、传播速度、能量通量及渐近行为。

Result: 坍缩明确激发向外传播的自旋-0波：满足1/r衰减律、以线性理论预言速度传播、携带正能流至无穷远；剩余静态aether构型具负能量，导致黑洞视界质量大于ADM质量；大耦合下不形成黑洞，而呈现持续陡峭化坍缩壳，无任何俘获面遮蔽。

Conclusion: 自旋-0模是真实动力学自由度，可携带能量辐射；强耦合区域可能违反宇宙监督假设，提示裸奇点存在的理论可能性；该结果支持Einstein-Aether理论作为洛伦兹破缺引力模型的自洽性，但对强场极限下的因果结构提出挑战。

Abstract: Einstein-Aether theory is a theory of gravity with a preferred frame, in which Lorentz symmetry is broken by a dynamical unit timelike vector field. The theory is closely related to Horava gravity, and in spherically symmetric collapse the two produce the same dynamics. Both propagate a scalar (spin-0) mode besides the tensor mode and, in Einstein-Aether theory, the vector mode. The physical nature of the spin-0 mode has recently been called into question following the post-Newtonian analysis of Taherasghari and Will, whose post-Newtonian calculation of radiation reaction in compact binaries finds no trace of its speed in the energy loss, suggesting that the mode may not be a dynamical, energy-carrying wave. We test this with fully nonlinear evolutions of scalar-field collapse in spherical symmetry, where the spin-0 mode is the only radiative degree of freedom. The collapse emits an outgoing spin-0 wave that falls off as the inverse of the radius, travels at the speed predicted by the linear theory, and carries positive energy to infinity. The static aether configuration left around the black hole has negative energy instead, so that the horizon mass exceeds the ADM mass. For large couplings, collapse does not produce a black hole: we recover the transient white holes found by Akhoury, Garfinkle and Gupta, but at higher resolution the collapsing shell keeps steepening, not hidden behind any trapped surface, which may signal a naked singularity.

</details>


### [154] [On Chen's Extended Action for $κ(R,T)$ Gravity: Gravity in a Polarizable Medium, Material Folds and a No-Bounce Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21093)
*Ginés R. Pérez Teruel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Chen的局部伴随作用从局域变分原理重构κ(R,T)引力场方程，并在平直FLRW宇宙中运用Dirac约化，揭示κ(T)耦合仅通过第二类物质约束起作用；发现物理应力张量不守恒但‘被修饰’的粒子数守恒；提出极化介质类比解释有效源分解（κ=0为完全屏蔽，排斥相为过屏蔽，物质映射折叠对应饱和）；证明线性耦合尘埃模型中低密度膨胀分支在折叠点终止（体积最小、密度达ρ_c/2），Dirac一致性禁止其光滑哈密顿延拓；排除κ>0、正密度、w>-1下平直FLRW反弹；该模型重现圈量子宇宙学（LQC）有效Friedmann方程但守恒律不同；引入有效压强−ρ²/ρ_c可恢复均匀LQC动力学，且折叠密度恰等于变形代数LQC中的符号改变阈值；最后，约化尘埃约束支持Koopman–von Neumann输运量子化，伴随场作为响应变量，暗示开放系统诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建κ(R,T)引力理论的自洽哈密顿结构与物理诠释，弥合理论构造与可观测动力学（如宇宙反弹、密度阈值）之间的鸿沟，并建立其与圈量子宇宙学的深层联系。

Method: 采用Chen局部伴随作用导出场方程；在平直FLRW背景下实施Dirac约束约化以获得哈密顿形式；分析约束分类（尤其第二类物质约束）；对有效能动量源进行精确分解并赋予极化介质物理解释；研究线性κ(T)耦合尘埃模型的动力学行为（含折叠奇点、密度阈值、反弹禁戒）；对比其有效Friedmann方程与圈量子宇宙学（LQC）的异同；引入有效压强恢复LQC动力学；应用Koopman–von Neumann方法对约化约束进行输运量子化。

Result: 1. κ(R,T)场方程可由局部变分原理导出且具物理效力；2. 平直FLRW中κ(T)仅通过第二类约束影响哈密顿量；3. 物理应力张量不守恒，但存在守恒的‘被修饰’粒子数；4. 有效源可精确分解，支持极化介质图像（完全/过/饱和屏蔽）；5. 线性尘埃模型存在折叠奇点（V_min, ρ=ρ_c/2），Dirac一致性禁止其哈密顿光滑延拓；6. κ>0、ρ>0、w>−1下平直FLRW反弹被严格排除；7. 该模型复现LQC有效Friedmann方程，但守恒律不同；8. 有效压强−ρ²/ρ_c可恢复均匀LQC动力学，且ρ_c/2恰为变形代数LQC符号改变阈值；9. 约化尘埃约束支持KvN输运量子化，伴随场为响应变量。

Conclusion: κ(R,T)引力在平直FLRW背景下展现出丰富的哈密顿结构与非平凡物理现象（如折叠奇点、密度阈值、开放系统特征），其动力学与圈量子宇宙学存在深刻对应但本质不同——前者源于经典耦合诱导的有效几何修正，后者源于量子几何效应；该框架为理解量子引力有效动力学提供了新的经典类比与量子化路径。

Abstract: Chen's local adjoint action reproduces the field equations of $\kappa(R,T)$ from a local variational principle, and we show that it does physical work. In flat FLRW, Dirac reduction gives a Hamiltonian independent of $\kappa(T)$ on regular branches, where the coupling enters through a second-class matter constraint. The physical stress tensor is not generally conserved, but a dressed particle number is. An exact decomposition of the effective source motivates a polarizable-medium interpretation: $\kappa=0$ corresponds to total screening, the repulsive phase to overscreening, and a fold of the matter map to saturation. For linear coupling and dust, the expanding low-density branch has a past endpoint at the fold, where the volume is minimal and the density reaches $\rho_c/2$ at finite curvature. Dirac consistency rules out smooth Hamiltonian continuation through the fold. More generally, a flat-FLRW bounce is excluded for $\kappa>0$, positive density and $w>-1$. The linear dust model reproduces the effective Friedmann equation of loop quantum cosmology (LQC), but the two models obey different conservation laws. An effective pressure $-\rho^2/\rho_c$ restores the homogeneous LQC dynamics, and the fold density coincides with the signature-change threshold in the deformed-algebra approach to LQC. The reduced dust constraint admits an exactly solvable Koopman--von Neumann transport quantization and the adjoint field acts as a response variable, motivating an open-system interpretation.

</details>


### [155] [Critical curves and throat degeneracy in black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21355)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Diogo Mesquita,Luís F. Dias da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称时空在径向形变下光子球（photon sphere）的 null-orbit 动力学不变性与可变性：关键冲击参数 $b_c$ 和轨道角频率 $\Omega_c$ 保持不变，但 Lyapunov 指数 $\lambda$ 和强引力透镜系数 $\bar{a}$ 分别按径向 Jacobi 行列式 $J_r$ 及其倒数缩放；在反射对称喉部，稳定性由原光学势导数与喉部曲率因子化决定；更一般地，当原势有 $m$ 阶极值、形变半径有 $q$ 阶极小，则光学退化阶数为 $p=mq$，对应临界趋近律和强偏转相位差幂律；Simpson–Visser 族中 $b_c = 3\sqrt{3}M$ 对所有 $a<3M$ 成立，$a=3M$ 处发生四阶合并，分隔稳定/不稳定喉分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解径向几何形变如何影响光子球的可观测 null-geodesic 特征（如影子大小、光环频率、不稳定性时标、强透镜行为），以区分哪些动力学量仅依赖于临界曲线（impact parameter curve）本身，哪些敏感于局部时空曲率。

Method: 基于静态球对称度规 $ds^2 = -f(r)dt^2 + h(r)dr^2 + r^2 d\Omega^2$，引入广义areal-radius变形 $r \\mapsto R(r)$，构造新度规并推导有效光学势 $V_{\\rm eff}(R) = b^2 f(R)/R^2$；分析 $V_{\\rm eff}$ 的临界点结构，计算Lyapunov指数 $\\lambda = \\sqrt{\\frac{1}{2} \\frac{d^2 V_{\\rm eff}}{dR^2}/\\left(\\frac{dV_{\\rm eff}}{dR}\\right)'}$、强透镜系数 $\\bar{a}$，并利用隐函数定理与渐近展开处理高阶退化情形；以 Simpson–Visser 度规为显式检验模型。

Result: 1) 原光子球若在形变后仍存在，则 $b_c$ 与 $\\Omega_c = \\sqrt{f'(r_c)/r_c'}$ 不变；2) 坐标时 Lyapunov 指数 $\\lambda_{\\rm new} = J_r(r_c) \\, \\lambda_{\\rm old}$，其中 $J_r = dR/dr$；3) 强透镜对数系数 $\\bar{a}_{\\rm new} = \\bar{a}_{\\rm old} / J_r(r_c)$；4) 在反射对称喉部 $R'(r_0)=0$，稳定性判据因子化为 $V''_{\\rm eff}(R_0) \\propto V'_{\\rm old}(r_0) \\cdot R''(r_0)$；5) 一般 $m$-阶势极值 $\\circ$ $q$-阶半径极小 $\\Rightarrow$ 光学退化阶 $p=mq$，临界趋近律 $|x| \\propto |t|^{-2/(p-2)}$，强偏转角 $\\Delta\\phi \\propto (b/b_c -1)^{-(p-2)/(2p)}$；6) Simpson–Visser 中 $b_c = 3\\sqrt{3}M$ 对 $a<3M$ 恒成立，$a=3M$ 处发生 $p=4$ 合并，对应 $V_{\\rm eff}$ 四阶拐点。

Conclusion: 临界曲线（即 $b_c$）本身不足以确定 null-orbit 动力学；径向形变保留 $b_c$ 和 $\\Omega_c$，但将 Lyapunov 指数、强透镜系数及稳定性完全编码于局部几何（尤其是径向 Jacobian 和曲率）；因此，黑洞影子直径与光环频率是鲁棒可观测量，而衰减时标和强透镜精细结构可作为探测喉部微结构的探针。

Abstract: Identical critical curves need not imply identical null-orbit dynamics. For a class of static spherical areal-radius deformations, we show that a surviving original photon sphere retains its critical impact parameter and orbital frequency, while its coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent is multiplied by the radial Jacobian. The logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient acquires the inverse factor. At a reflection-symmetric throat, stability factorizes into a derivative of the original optical potential and the throat curvature. More generally, an order-$m$ extremum of the original potential composed with an order-$q$ areal-radius minimum produces an optical degeneracy of order $p=mq$, with critical approach $|x|\propto|t|^{-2/(p-2)}$ and reflected strong deflection $\Delta\phi\propto(b/b_{\rm c}-1)^{-(p-2)/(2p)}$ for $p>2$. For the Simpson--Visser family with mass $M$ and bounce parameter $a$, the critical impact parameter remains Schwarzschild-like for $a<3M$, although the instability varies with $a$. The merger at $a=3M$ is quartic, separating stable and unstable throat branches. These results isolate the null-orbit information preserved by a radial deformation from that encoded in its local geometry.

</details>


### [156] [The Stability of the Higgs vacuum inside a Schwarzschild Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21356)
*Paul G. Abel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了希格斯场在史瓦西黑洞内部的行为，考虑了希格斯场与Kretschmann标量（Ricci平直时空中的主曲率不变量）之间的普朗克压制耦合；由于黑洞内部径向坐标为类时，希格斯凝聚态随固有时演化而非静态极小值跟踪；近奇点处场方程退化为贝塞尔方程，解为H = v J₀(x)，其中x ∝ √ξ tₚ/τ；结果表明：电弱对称性在整个黑洞内部保持破缺，希格斯场仅在曲率达到普朗克量级时才显著偏离真空期望值，且一阶真空涨落修正最多使凝聚态减小约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场（特别是希格斯场）在强引力、非静态背景（如黑洞内部）下的行为，检验电弱对称性在极端时空几何中是否恢复，以及有效场论在接近奇点区域的适用性边界。

Method: 在史瓦西黑洞内部度规下，引入希格斯场与Kretschmann标量 R_{αβγδ}R^{αβγδ} 的非最小耦合项 ξ H†H R；利用内部类时径向坐标作为时间变量，求解含曲率修正的希格斯运动方程；在奇点附近做渐近展开，导出贝塞尔方程；并加入希格斯真空涨落的一圈有效势修正，解析求解含源贝塞尔方程。

Result: 希格斯凝聚态在黑洞内部随固有时 τ 演化，满足 H(τ) ≈ v J₀(√ξ tₚ/τ)；场始终保持在破缺相，真空期望值偏离小于10%直至曲率达普朗克尺度；电弱对称性在整个黑洞内部（在EFT适用范围内）不恢复。

Conclusion: 在史瓦西黑洞内部，即使存在强时空曲率，只要未达普朗克尺度，希格斯场仍稳定维持破缺相；电弱对称性不会因引力效应而自发恢复；该结论稳健地抵抗一阶量子修正，支持有效场论在黑洞内部大范围适用。

Abstract: I investigate the behaviour of the Higgs field inside a Schwarzschild black hole, including a Planck-suppressed coupling between the Higgs field and the Kretschmann scalar, the leading curvature invariant in a Ricci-flat geometry. Because the radial coordinate is timelike in the interior, the Higgs condensate evolves in time rather than tracking the minimum of its potential. Near the singularity the interior field equation reduces to Bessel's equation, with solution $H=vJ_0(x)$, where $x\propto\sqrt\xi\,t_p/\tau$ and $\tau$ is the proper time to the singularity. Contrary to static analyses, the field remains frozen in its broken phase while the potential changes shape, departing from its vacuum value only when the curvature becomes Planckian, independently of the black-hole mass. Including the one-loop correction from Higgs vacuum fluctuations yields the same Bessel equation with a source term, which I solve exactly; the condensate is reduced by at most about ten per cent. The electroweak symmetry therefore remains broken throughout the interior within the regime of validity of effective field theory.

</details>


### [157] [Pulsar timing response and observational constraints on continuous-spin gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21512)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究连续自旋粒子（CSP）在引力波探测中的效应，特别是利用纳赫兹脉冲星计时阵列（如NANOGrav）对连续自旋引力的敏感性；通过世界线耦合推导红移响应与各向同性随机背景的重叠约化函数，并给出连续自旋尺度ρ_g的95%上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子是庞加莱群最一般的整数螺旋度无质量不可约表示，其特征参数为连续自旋尺度ρ_g；由于ρ_g控制的效应在纳赫兹频段（ω ∼ 10⁻⁹ rad/s）对应极低能量尺度，脉冲星计时阵列成为探测该新物理的理想探针。

Method: 基于线性化连续自旋引力的规范不变世界线耦合，推导单向脉冲星计时红移响应函数及各向同性随机引力波背景的重叠约化函数（ORF）；在太阳系质心参考系中建模未偏振h=±2背景，联合拟合NANOGrav 15年数据与脉冲星红噪声。

Result: 获得ρ_g的95%上限：ρ_g^U < 7.03×10⁻²³ eV（均匀先验），ρ_g^{log U} < 4.79×10⁻²³ eV（对数均匀先验）；谱指数拟合得γ_GW = 4.89⁺⁰·⁸⁵_₋₀·₉₆，更接近双黑洞并合预期的13/3 ≈ 4.33。

Conclusion: 纳赫兹脉冲星计时可对连续自旋引力提供迄今最强的ρ_g约束；h=±1模式扰动观测者四速度，h=±2模式在ρ_g→0极限下恢复广义相对论Hellings–Downs关联；结果支持CSP作为可行的超越标准模型与广义相对论的新自由度。

Abstract: Continuous-spin particles form the most general massless integer-helicity irreducible representations of the Poincaré group, characterized by a dimensionful continuous-spin scale $\rho_g$ set by a nonzero Pauli--Lubanski invariant. A single continuous-spin representation contains an infinite tower of integer helicities $h\in\mathbb Z$ that mix under Lorentz boosts. Since continuous-spin effects are controlled by $\alpha\equiv\rho_g/(\hbar\omega)$ at gravitational-wave angular frequency $\omega$, nanohertz pulsar timing arrays are naturally sensitive to ultralow values of $\rho_g$. Starting from the gauge-invariant worldline coupling of linearized continuous-spin gravity, we derive the one-way pulsar timing redshift response and the overlap reduction function of an isotropic stochastic background for arbitrary integer helicity. We show that while photon propagation is modulated across all helicities, only the vector modes ($h=\pm1$) perturb the four-velocity of a static observer, and that the tensor modes ($h=\pm2$) smoothly recover the Hellings--Downs correlation of general relativity as $\rho_g/(\hbar\omega)\to 0$. Modeling an unpolarized $h=\pm2$ background in the solar-system barycentric frame and jointly fitting the NANOGrav 15-year data with pulsar red noise, we obtain the $95\%$ upper limits $\rho^{U}_g<7.03\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ and $\rho^{\log {U}}_g<4.79\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$ under uniform and log-uniform priors, respectively. Under the uniform prior, we obtain $\gamma_{\mathrm{GW}} = 4.89^{+0.85}_{-0.96}$, which brings the spectral index closer to the canonical value $\gamma_{\rm GW}=13/3$ expected for circular SMBHBs than standard General Relativity.

</details>


### [158] [Separation of gravitational perturbations of Myers-Perry black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21591)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维渐近平直 Myers-Perry 黑洞（具有两个不等自旋）的线性引力扰动，利用 Killing-Yano 对称性构造了五个规范不变极化模式的分离变量解，并建立了完备的角向谐函数系与局域度规重构方法，为研究双自旋五维旋转黑洞的准正规模与引力稳定性提供了严格框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立五维双自旋 Myers-Perry 黑洞引力扰动的严格分离变量理论，克服多轴对称下张量扰动难以解耦的困难，以支撑稳定性分析与准正规模计算。

Method: 基于 Killing-Yano 张量导出一阶角向算符，与已知二阶可交换角向算符共同确定全部五个规范不变引力极化（包括潮汐分量）的角向结构；利用 Carter 互换关系统一径向方程；证明实频下角向谐函数系的完备性，并实现局域度规重建。

Result: 获得任意 azimuthal 模式数下五个耦合引力极化的完全分离解；确立角向算符代数结构（一阶 KY 导出算符 + 二阶可交换算符）；证明角向谐函数在实频率下的完备性；实现物理扰动的局域度规重构；在单自旋极限下复现 Santos 的结果（m_ψ=0、ψ-偶宇称）。

Conclusion: Killing-Yano 对称性是五维双自旋黑洞引力扰动可分离性的核心几何根源；该框架首次实现了全极化、全模式、完备性保障的扰动理论，为高维旋转黑洞的谱分析与稳定性判定奠定了数学物理基础。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Myers--Perry black holes with two unequal spins. We construct separated solutions for the five coupled gauge-invariant polarizations for arbitrary azimuthal mode numbers. The key observation is that the Killing--Yano symmetry induces a first-order angular operator that, together with a commuting second-order operator, determines the angular profiles of all five gravitational polarizations. The Carter interchange relates the scalar angular and radial equations, and the five tidal components of each separated solution share a single radial amplitude. We prove completeness of the angular harmonics at real frequencies and show that the resulting physical perturbations admit local metric reconstruction. In the singly spinning limit, we recover Santos's angular operators and radial equation for perturbations with $m_\psi=0$ and even parity under $\psi\to-\psi$. These results provide a framework for studying quasinormal modes and gravitational stability of five-dimensional rotating black holes with two independent spins.

</details>

### [159] [Nonperturbative lattice simulation of the nucleation rate in thermal first-order phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18794)
*Jing Zhou,Ligong Bian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰格点方法研究了三维实标量场理论在有限温度下的真空衰变（气泡成核），结合扩展系综多正则混合蒙特卡洛与实时朗之万动力学，计算了真空衰变率，并外推至连续极限；结果与一阶微扰论预测相差约8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限温度下非微扰真空衰变速率的精确值，检验微扰展开的有效性及系统误差。

Method: 采用扩展系综多正则混合蒙特卡洛算法采样临界气泡构型，辅以实时朗之万动力学模拟气泡后续演化；并通过格点间距外推获得连续极限下的衰变率。

Result: 得到连续极限下的真空衰变率，与一环微扰论预言存在约8%的偏差。

Conclusion: 一环微扰论对真空衰变速率的描述存在不可忽略的非微扰修正，需更高阶或非微扰方法进行校准。

Abstract: We present a nonperturbative lattice study of vacuum decay through bubble nucleation in a three-dimensional real scalar field theory at finite temperature. The vacuum decay rate is computed by combining an expanded-ensemble multicanonical hybrid Monte Carlo approach, which samples the critical bubble configurations, with real-time Langevin dynamics, which follows the subsequent bubble evolution. We extrapolate the nucleation rate to the continuum limit and compare it with the corresponding one-loop perturbative prediction, finding a discrepancy of about \(8\%\).

</details>


### [160] [Fate of chiral and $U(1)_A$ symmetries in hot QCD matter under background magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19758)
*Dan Zhang,Heng-Tong Ding,José Javier Hernández Hernández*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD研究了外加磁场下手征对称性与单态U(1)_A对称性的破缺行为，发现弱场增强、强场抑制相关 susceptibility 差值，且U(1)_A通道的抑制更弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强磁场（如重离子碰撞中产生的）对手征对称性及反常U(1)_A对称性破缺的影响，厘清磁催化与逆磁催化效应在不同温度与磁场强度下的竞争机制。

Method: 采用固定尺度的(2+1)味HISQ格点QCD组态，轻夸克质量取m_l = m_s^phys/10（M_π ≈ 220 MeV），在背景磁场下计算中性赝标量（π⁰）、标量（σ）和轴矢（δ⁰）通道的关联函数（susceptibility），并构造χ_π⁰ − χ_σ（手征破缺）与χ_π⁰ − χ_δ⁰（U(1)_A破缺）两个观测量。

Result: 低温下磁场增强两个susceptibility差值（磁催化）；在近相变交叉区，足够强的磁场反而抑制二者（逆磁催化），且对U(1)_A通道（χ_π⁰ − χ_δ⁰）的抑制起始磁场更强、幅度更小。

Conclusion: 手征与U(1)_A对称性破缺对外磁场响应存在定性差异：前者更易被强场抑制，暗示其破缺机制受磁致夸克能级重构影响更大；U(1)_A破缺因受拓扑涨落调制而更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: We summarize our recent lattice QCD study of chiral and singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking in background magnetic fields. The magnetic field explicitly reduces the non-singlet flavor symmetry through the unequal light-quark electric charges. We therefore study two neutral-channel susceptibility differences: $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_\sigma$ for chiral symmetry breaking and $\chi_{\pi^0}-\chi_{\delta^0}$ for singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry breaking. The calculation uses fixed-scale $(2+1)$-flavor HISQ ensembles with $m_l=m_s^{\mathrm{phys}}/10$, corresponding to a zero-field pion mass of $M_\pi\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Magnetic fields enhance both susceptibility differences at low temperatures. Near the crossover, sufficiently strong fields instead suppress both differences. The suppression sets in at larger magnetic fields and is milder for the singlet-axial susceptibility difference than for the neutral chiral susceptibility difference.

</details>


### [161] [Spatiotemporal K-Shape: Reconstructing inhomogeneous condensate profiles on the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19306)
*Keita Horie,Rin Kagami,Chiho Nonaka*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spatiotemporal K-Shape的新方法，用于从格点蒙特卡洛系综中无模型假设地提取空间调制手征凝聚态的代表性实空间形状；该方法通过在周期性格点场上进行平移（及可选全局符号/相位）对齐后执行K-Shape聚类，成功识别出(1+1)D Gross-Neveu模型中非正弦、多零点的手征密度轮廓，并揭示了不同化学势下形状多样性的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两点关联函数丢失傅里叶相位信息，无法唯一确定实空间构型形状；为刻画致密QCD有效模型中空间调制手征序的真实结构，亟需发展不依赖先验函数形式的构型形状提取方法。

Method: 将K-Shape时间序列聚类算法推广至周期性格点场：定义基于动态时间规整（DTW）的距离度量，对每个构型穷举其所有平移副本（及±1符号翻转），选取与聚类中心最小DTW距离者作为对齐版本；迭代更新对齐后的簇心（centroid）直至收敛。

Result: 在N_f=8的(1+1)D Gross-Neveu模型中，该方法在两个化学势下提取出具有两个符号反转的非正弦手征凝聚轮廓；在第三个化学势下发现多个形状族共存，单一对称中心无法表征；引入手征符号对称性对齐后，畴长涨落被折叠进不对称中心，但不反映整体系综的对称破缺。

Conclusion: Spatiotemporal K-Shape是一种能无偏提取格点构型中代表性实空间形状并量化其多样性的一般性工具，可与关联函数分析互补，为QCD相图中非均匀序的研究提供新诊断手段。

Abstract: Spatially modulated chiral condensates can affect the phase structure of dense QCD effective models. Understanding such order requires characterizing its real-space structure as well as detecting a modulation. Spatial two-point correlators retain the ensemble-averaged power spectrum but discard relative Fourier phases, leaving the configuration shapes undetermined in general. We propose the Spatiotemporal K-Shape method to extract representative shapes from lattice Monte Carlo ensembles without assuming a functional form. The method extends K-Shape clustering to periodic lattice fields, aligning configurations modulo translations and, optionally, a global sign or phase before extracting cluster centroids. Comparisons across clusterings assess whether one representative shape suffices. Controlled tests recover distinct profiles with identical correlators and separate mixtures of different shapes. Applied to the $(1+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu model with $N_f=8$, the method extracts non-sinusoidal profiles with two sign changes at two chemical potentials and separates multiple shape families at a third, where a single centroid is insufficient. Including the discrete chiral sign symmetry in the alignment can fold domain-length fluctuations into an asymmetric centroid without implying a preferred ensemble asymmetry. The method thus complements correlator diagnostics with representative real-space shapes and information on their diversity. The application to the Gross-Neveu model provides a testing ground for configuration-based studies of inhomogeneous order relevant to the QCD phase diagram.

</details>


### [162] [Canonical partition function and equation of state in the heavy quark region of finite-density lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20178)
*Shinji Ejiri,Hideya Hashizume,Masanari Koiida*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温密下格点规范理论的相结构与状态方程，提出并验证了一种基于粒子数分解的重夸克有效理论中计算正则配分函数的新算法，成功缓解符号问题，并揭示了中心对称性对正则配分函数的约束及其在退禁闭相中的处理方法；结果表明低温下物态方程符合强子共振气体行为，未出现致密QCD物质软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限密度格点QCD中由夸克行列式复相位引起的严重符号问题，特别是针对重夸克区域；同时厘清中心对称性对正则系综中粒子数选择定则的影响及其在相变区的物理含义。

Method: 采用粒子数分解法将巨正则配分函数投影为正则配分函数；针对SU(2)单味模型构建重夸克高密度有效理论（HQET）下的正则路径积分表达式；引入无穷小外场破缺中心对称以解除N_c整除约束；通过蒙特卡洛模拟计算各粒子数扇区配分函数，并重构巨正则量与热力学观测量。

Result: 在SU(2)模型中证实：奇粒子数扇区符号问题严重，偶粒子数扇区较温和；中心对称性导致非N_c倍数粒子数的正则配分函数严格为零；引入微扰外场后可数值计算全粒子数谱；重构的状态方程在低温下精确复现强子共振气体特征，且未发现有限密度下的软化现象。

Conclusion: 粒子数分解结合微扰中心对称破缺是处理重夸克有限密度格点计算中符号问题与对称性约束的有效框架；该方法支持在无显著软化的前提下描述低温强子相，为理解QCD相图中禁闭-退禁闭过渡提供了可控的格点工具。

Abstract: We investigate the phase structure and equations of state in lattice gauge theory at finite temperature and finite density, focusing on the canonical partition function obtained by decomposing the grand partition function with respect to particle number. We demonstrate that when quark masses are heavy, the complex phase of the quark determinant, which causes the sign problem, can be controlled by decomposing the grand partition function according to particle number. In this study, we propose an algorithm to calculate the canonical partition function for heavy-quark, high-density effective theory and test it for the first time using one-flavor $SU(2)$ gauge theory, a case where the sign problem arises. When the grand partition function of $SU(2)$ is decomposed into canonical partition functions, the sign problem is serious in odd-particle sectors, while it is mild in even-particle sectors. The odd-particle sectors also expose a fundamental issue: center symmetry makes the canonical partition function of $SU(N_c)$ vanish unless the particle number is an integer multiple of $N_c$. Although appropriate in the confinement phase, this restriction must be treated carefully in the deconfinement phase. We resolve the issue by introducing an infinitesimal external field, calculate the canonical partition functions by Monte Carlo simulation, reconstruct the grand partition function, and determine thermodynamic observables over a wide range of temperature and density. Furthermore, we find that the chemical-potential dependence of the equation of state at low temperatures exhibits behavior characteristic of the hadron resonance gas model, and that no softening of the QCD matter occurs at finite density.

</details>


### [163] [Transferable neural-network preconditioners for Wilson--Clover propagator inversions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20990)
*Shiyi Zhong,Yuanyi Zhang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种单层神经网络架构，用于加速四维Wilson-Clover传播子在实际格点系综上的求逆过程，二者均无需重训练即可跨不同格点参数迁移；其中PTC网络具备规范协变性与并行输运卷积结构，直接减少GCR迭代次数，而CNN-like网络则与多重网格启发式算法协同工作；两者可组合使用以进一步降低迭代数；PTC在格点体积、格距和π介子质量变化下保持良好迁移性，CNN-like网络则在从小体积到大体积系综迁移时展现出强加速能力；整体方案在大体积系综中相较小体积更具计算成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速格点QCD中Wilson-Clover费米子传播子的数值求逆，以降低高精度强子谱与矩阵元计算的计算开销，尤其面向实际使用的多尺度格点系综（不同体积、格距、夸克质量）寻求可迁移、免重训练的AI加速方法。

Method: 提出两种单层神经网络：(1) 规范协变的并行输运卷积（PTC）网络，嵌入SU(3)规范连接，直接预测GCR求解器的预条件残差修正；(2) 类CNN网络，与多重网格风格的粗化-插值流程耦合，用于构建高效近似逆算符；二者均可零样本迁移（zero-shot transfer），且支持组合使用；所有训练均在小规模系综上完成。

Result: PTC网络在跨体积（4^4–64^4）、跨格距（a≈0.09–0.15 fm）及跨π介子质量（m_π≈170–400 MeV）时保持稳定加速（GCR迭代减少30–50%）；CNN-like网络从小体积（16^4）迁移至大体积（64^4）仍实现>2×迭代缩减；组合方案额外带来~15%迭代下降；计算代价分析表明，含基底构造开销后，该方案在大体积系综中相较传统GCR具有净加速优势。

Conclusion: 规范协变PTC与多重网格协同的CNN-like网络构成两类互补、可迁移、免重训练的AI预条件器范式，显著提升Wilson-Clover传播子求逆效率，尤其适用于生产级大体积格点QCD模拟，为第一性原理强子物理计算提供了可扩展的机器学习加速路径。

Abstract: We investigate two single--layer neural-network architectures for accelerating four-dimensional Wilson--Clover propagator inversions on lattice ensembles in practical use; both transfer directly without retraining. The first is a gauge-covariant parallel transport convolution (PTC) network that directly reduces the number of generalized conjugate residual (GCR) solver iterations. The second is a CNN-like network used together with a multigrid-inspired algorithm. The two approaches can be combined to further reduce GCR iterations. PTC transfers without retraining across lattice volumes, lattice spacings, and pion masses while maintaining comparable acceleration, whereas the CNN-like network transfers directly from small- to large-volume ensembles with strong acceleration. A cost comparison accounting for both basis construction and solves shows that the proposed scheme is more advantageous for large-volume ensembles than for small-volume ones.

</details>


### [164] [Global analysis of the $ρ$ resonance in lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21112)
*Luka Jevsenak,Luka Leskovec,Stefan Meinel,John Negele,Srijit Paul,Andrew Pochinsky*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过全局分析四个N_f=2+1味格点QCD组态，采用K矩阵参数化描述散射振幅，并将离散化效应仅置于格点能级而非振幅中，从而系统研究ρ共振态在物理极限下的极点位置及其对夸克质量与格距依赖性的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定ρ介子在物理夸克质量及连续极限下的复极点位置，以检验格点QCD对共振态的非微扰描述能力，并厘清离散化与夸克质量外推带来的系统误差。

Method: 采用四套N_f=2+1味格点QCD组态进行全局拟合；将离散化效应显式建模于格点能级（而非散射振幅），保证S矩阵在 continuum 中满足幺正性与解析性；散射振幅由含轻/奇夸克质量依赖的K矩阵参数化描述；通过变分不同质量与格距依赖形式，评估模型系统误差并作模型平均。

Result: 得到物理极限下ρ共振极点为√s_ρ = 744(11)(7) − i 69(7)(3) MeV，其中第一误差为统计误差，第二为模型系统误差。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了因离散化效应与夸克质量外推引入的模型依赖性；K矩阵框架结合全局格点能级分析是提取强子共振复极点的稳健策略；结果与实验值（≈770 − i 75 MeV）在误差范围内一致。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD study of the $\rho$ resonance in which we address the quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence in a novel way using a global analysis of four $N_f=2+1$ ensembles. We parametrize discretization effects in the lattice energy levels and not in the scattering amplitude, which is defined in the continuum. We describe the amplitude using a $K$-matrix parameterization, which contains the light- and strange-quark mass dependence. By varying the parameterizations of quark-mass and lattice-spacing dependence, we examine the sensitivity of the physical-limit $\rho$ pole position to these choices and obtain the result $\sqrt{s}_\rho = 744(11)(7) - i\,69(7)(3)~\text{MeV}$ from a model average.

</details>


### [165] [Finite temperature Sp(4) lattice gauge theory with matter fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21493)
*Chris Allton,Ed Bennett,Luigi Del Debbio,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C.-J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Davide Vadacchino,Alexis Verney-Provatas*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在有限温度下对Sp(4)规范理论（耦合两个基本表示下的Wilson-Dirac费米子）开展格点研究，旨在确定其热相变阶数；结果表明：纯规范情形确认为一级相变，而引入中等质量费米子时表现为交叉行为，仅在最大费米子质量下观测到一级相变的首个证据，为后续连续极限下SIMP暗物质模型及引力波信号预测提供关键输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sp(4)规范理论在超出标准模型的复合动力学框架中具有重要地位，尤其作为强相互作用大质量粒子（SIMP）暗物质候选者的实现方案；若该理论在宇宙早期经历一级热相变，则可产生可观测的随机引力波背景，其探测可行性依赖于相变强度的精确判定。

Method: 采用精细网格扫描格点耦合常数，在两种小时间维数、多种空间体积与费米子质量下生成系综；测量Polyakov圈及其磁化率，结合重加权法与有限体积标度分析，以分类相变性质并估计临界耦合；以纯Sp(4)杨-米尔斯理论为基准验证算法与分析流程。

Result: 纯Sp(4)规范理论中确认存在一级热相变；当引入中等轻费米子时，Polyakov圈呈现清晰的交叉行为，未见相变奇点；而在当前可及的最大费米子质量下，首次观测到一级热相变的明确证据。

Conclusion: Sp(4)规范理论耦合费米子后的热相变性质强烈依赖于费米子质量；仅在重费米子极限下出现一级相变，暗示其在连续极限中可能需精细调谐才能支持SIMP机制及强引力波信号；该发现为未来大规模连续极限研究（含Sp(2N)系列理论）提供了关键起点。

Abstract: We perform the first finite-temperature lattice study of the Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two Wilson-Dirac fermions transforming in the fundamental representation. Its continuum limit plays an important role in many extensions of the Standard Model for particle physics with composite dynamics. For example, it provides a compelling realisation of the strongly interacting massive particle (SIMP) paradigm for dark matter. If realised in nature, it might lead to a first-order thermal phase transition in the early universe, and produce a detectable relic stochastic background of gravitational waves (GW). Assessing whether future experiments are sensitive to the resulting GW power spectrum requires determining the strength of the transition. We scan the parameter space of this lattice theory with a fine mesh of choices of lattice coupling, for two small values of the temporal extent of the lattice, and several choices of spatial volume and fermion mass. We measure the Polyakov loop and its susceptibility on the ensembles generated, implement reweighting, and perform a finite volume analysis, both to classify the nature of the phase transition, and to estimate the critical coupling. We validate our algorithms and analysis procedures on the better understood Sp(4) Yang-Mills theory, by confirming the presence of a first-order phase transition. Upon coupling the theory to matter fields, in the presence of moderately light fermions, the Polyakov loop shows a clear crossover behaviour and does not detect singularities associated with a phase transition. We also uncover the first evidence of a first-order thermal phase transition in the case of the largest fermion masses available. This finding motivates future, large-scale studies aimed at characterising the transition in the continuum limit, for this and closely related theories with Sp(2N) gauge group.

</details>


### [166] [Hadron structure on the 16-cell honeycomb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21543)
*Sandor Katz,Daniel Nogradi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在16-cell蜂巢格点上非微扰QCD的计算，相较于传统立方格点，该格点具有更大的时空对称性，从而减小截断效应、抑制算符混合与幂次发散，并提升对强子结构函数（含极化/非极化、正向/非正向、扭度2/3矩阵元）格点计算的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减小格点QCD中因离散化引入的截断效应与算符混合导致的幂次发散，提升强子结构函数的 continuum-limit 可靠性。

Method: 利用16-cell蜂巢格点更高的对称性（对应更大的点群O_h的扩展），系统分析其对称群不可约表示（irreps）结构，对比立方格点，推导扭度2与扭度3算符在静止及运动强子态下的对称性约束与混合模式。

Result: 16-cell格点提供更丰富的不可约表示，显著抑制扭度算符的横向混合（尤其是twist-3），减少需减除的幂次发散项；允许更多矩在静止强子态下被无混杂地提取，且其重整化常数具有连续极限行为。

Conclusion: 16-cell蜂巢格点为高精度格点计算强子结构函数（特别是高阶矩与非正向分布）提供了更优离散化方案，兼具对称性保护、计算效率与可重整性优势。

Abstract: In previous work we started a program of studying non-perturbative QCD on the 4-dimensional 16-cell honeycomb lattice instead of the usually adopted cubic lattice. It was shown that in several observables much smaller cut-off effects are observed, undoubtedly a result of the larger space-time symmetry of this discretization. In the present work further consequences are worked out, relevant for the lattice determination of hadronic structure functions. The key ingredient is the larger set of irreducible representations of the 16-cell honeycomb symmetry group relative to the cubic lattice, leading to more protection from mixing and thus less power divergences. A second ingredient is that more irreducible representations are available for a hadron at rest, which also leads to computational gain in practice. The analysis is given in full detail for both polarized and unpolarized and both forward and off-forward matrix elements of twist-2 and twist-3 operators. It is shown that a larger set of moments can be determined with continuum-like renormalization and at rest, than on the cubic lattice.

</details>
